- Clarification of the v0.3.0 integration-test isolation entry: the shipped mechanism is a hybrid, not the pure "each test owns its database" design that entry described. A per-file `setupFiles` hook resets the shared `THEOMEM_PG_URI` database to a clean clone of the migrated template before each file, while the dedicated `createIsolatedDb` clone is reserved for the heavy, state-sensitive files. Both reset BEFORE the file runs, so cross-file schema-state pollution is eliminated identically. See the integration-suite-stabilization plan ADR D1-a for the rationale.


### Security

- Workspace RBAC `/review` hardening (multi-tenant-rbac-and-slice1-completion, 7-agent review): closed a BLOCKER and the HIGH findings before merge. **Minted API keys now carry the minting admin's `workspace_id`** (the key-mint INSERT previously fell back to the schema default `'default'`, cross-tenant-misplacing every credential and decoupling the anti-escalation check from the key's runtime role). Caller-role resolution in the key-mint guard is now **default-deny** (`member`, never `owner`) on a membership-lookup miss and **fails closed with 503** on auth-backend error. Key **rotation preserves** the original key's `workspace_id` + `user_id` (was dropping both → role downgrade + workspace misplacement). The opt-in skill store's `insertDerivedSkill` now writes `workspace_id` (parity with the other skill inserts — was a cross-workspace leak on the public `VectorStore` surface). `requireScope` fails closed (503) on auth-backend error. The members last-owner transaction takes a single workspace-wide `FOR UPDATE` lock (deterministic order — closes a two-different-target concurrent-demotion deadlock). `/review` verdict: `READY_TO_MERGE`. See `.claude/knowledge-base/reviews/multi-tenant-rbac-and-slice1-completion-review-2026-06-18.md`.



### Known limitation (workspace isolation follow-up)

- RESOLVED (T3.1): the opt-in recall-cache, skill, and eviction surfaces are now fully workspace-isolated (per-workspace cache key + scope-WHERE threading). The earlier deferral to `multi-tenant-workspace-isolation-secondary-stores` is closed; they are now safe to enable in a multi-workspace deployment.
- Admin key management (`/v1/admin/keys/*`, `memory:admin` scope) is deployment-level in this slice — it manages every key in the deployment, not per-workspace. Per-workspace admin delegation belongs to the deferred membership layer; treat `memory:admin` as a deployment-operator privilege.
- The `AuthVerifier` compile-time `ScopedFilters` guard (ADR D4) is a forward-looking type, not yet wired into the store signatures — isolation is runtime-enforced via the `withWorkspace` binding + the `workspace_id NOT NULL` column (proven by the IDOR goal test). Tightening the store signatures to the compile-time guard is part of the same follow-up.

## [0.3.0] - 2026-06-16

### Added

- **theocloud-release-and-docker-rebuild** T2.2 — CI workflow dual-publishes Docker images to Docker Hub (`usetheo/themory`) and GHCR (`ghcr.io/usetheo/themory`) on tag push. Stable tags (x.y.z + x.y + latest) go to both registries; `edge` tag goes to GHCR only per ADR D2. Single combined push step ensures both registries receive the exact image digest Trivy scanned (EC-2).
- **theocloud-release-and-docker-rebuild** T3.1 — API key rotation procedure documented in README: zero-downtime create-then-revoke pattern + atomic `themory keys rotate <id>`.


### Changed

- **CLI/MCP warm-up cost documented** — the `themory server` / `themory mcp` handlers warm the Memory factory (real `LocalTransformerEmbedder` + `OpenAILLM`) on first use, a ~2-5s cold-start. Scope validation now fails fast (before the warm-up) so invalid requests do not pay the warm-up latency; the warm-up cost is documented for operators sizing health-probe timeouts.
- **integration-suite-stabilization** T0.1 — integration tests gain a per-file isolated-database helper (`tests/integration/_helpers/isolated-db.ts`). `global-setup.ts` now builds a migrated TEMPLATE database (`themem_template`) once per run; `createIsolatedDb({ embeddingDim? })` clones it via `CREATE DATABASE … TEMPLATE` (millisecond byte-clone, no migration replay) so each DB-touching test owns its database, removing the cross-file schema-state pollution that made shared-DB tests fail in the full suite while passing in isolation. Foundation for migrating the ~9 Bucket A files off the shared `themem` DB.
- **refactor-reflect-orchestrator** — `LocalScopedMemory.reflect`'s 166-line inline orchestration was extracted to a `this`-free `runReflection(deps, options)` in `packages/core/src/infrastructure/db/reflect-orchestrator.ts` (carrying `scopeToCounterTuple` + the two structural store-extension interfaces alongside their single consumer). `scoped-memory.ts` drops 545 → 352 LoC (under the 500-LoC `architecture.md` soft budget; closes the standing >500-LoC exception). Behaviour-preserving — no public API change; the db barrel re-export of `scopeToCounterTuple` is unchanged. Verified: typecheck clean, 930 contract tests green, 9/9 distillation/reflect integration tests green against real `ankane/pgvector` + real LLM (incl. `the-goal-test-llm-measured`), lint 0 warnings.
- **complete-workspace-publish-topology** — Publish topology restored for the workspace reorg (ADR D1-a src-only + D2-a no-npm-bin, owner decisions 2026-06-15). `packages/core` (`@usetheo/memory`, the only publishable package) gains a src-only `files` allow-list + `publish:check`; the publish/pack tests (`publish-readiness`, `pack-manifest`, `full-public-surface`) were retargeted from the private workspace root to `packages/core`.
- **complete-workspace-publish-topology** T4 — BREAKING (pre-release): the `@usetheo/memory/agent-tools` subpath is REDEFINED to a new leaf package `@usetheo/memory-agent-core`. The shared agent-runtime helpers (scope binding, env validation, Memory wiring, error classification, types) were extracted from `packages/cli` to `packages/agent-core` (depends only on `@usetheo/memory` + pg + zod). This breaks the former `cli↔mcp` import cycle (`packages/mcp` now imports the leaf, not the cli barrel) and makes the surface resolve via real Node `exports` instead of 3 build-time shims (ADR D3).
- **standardize-project-structure** T3.1 — Package manager migrated from npm to pnpm 10.34.1. `pnpm-workspace.yaml` created, `packageManager` field added to package.json, Dockerfile updated to use `corepack enable && pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`.
- **standardize-project-structure** T3.2 — REST API server extracted from `src/server/` to `packages/api/` as `@usetheo/memory-api` workspace package. Server-specific deps (Hono, @hono/zod-openapi) scoped to api package. Subpath export `./server` removed; consumers import from `@usetheo/memory-api`.
- **code-review-fixes-v0-2-1** T1.2 — BREAKING: `createApp` throws when `NODE_ENV=production` and `THEMORY_CORS_ORIGIN` is unset. Previously defaulted to wildcard `*` with a stderr warning. Development/test mode unchanged (OWASP A01:2021, ADR D2, EC-2).
- **theocloud-release-and-docker-rebuild** T2.1 — `docker-compose.prod.yaml` default image updated from `ghcr.io/usetheo/themory:0.1.0-alpha` to `usetheo/themory:0.2.0` (Docker Hub primary per ADR D1 + EC-3 alignment).
- **theocloud-release-and-docker-rebuild** T3.2 — README Docker section leads with Docker Hub (`docker pull usetheo/themory:0.2.0`); GHCR noted as alternative. All `0.1.0-alpha` references removed from README and Dockerfile.
- **cross-validation-roadmap** — Roadmap (README + CLAUDE.md) extended with 5 post-P0 backlog items derived from the `/loop-cross-validation` run against a reference project (2026-06-15): agentic tool-use + injection sandbox, layered config + `themory config`, optional cross-encoder reranker DIP, embedder registry + more providers, ingest robustness. All FROZEN under PRIORITY 0 (Inquebrável Rule 10) — documented for traceability, not active work. Two in-scope hardening candidates also flagged (`packages/mcp` build breakage; test-pyramid + dead-error audit). Watcher daemon explicitly discarded.


### Fixed

- **`@usetheo/memory` unresolvable by Node-SSR consumers (consumer-DX regression)** — the package shipped `src/**/*.ts` only (ADR D1-a src-only `files`), with `exports` pointing at the raw `.ts`. A Node-SSR consumer (TheoKit / Next / any Vite-SSR app) externalizes the dependency and hands it to Node, which cannot execute raw TypeScript — so `import "@usetheo/memory"` failed with `Failed to resolve entry for package "@usetheo/memory"`. Fix: `packages/core` now builds and ships `dist/**` (JS + `.d.ts` + sourcemaps) and `exports`/`types` point at the built `dist` files; a new `build:core` step (chained into the root `build`, plus a self-contained `packages/core` `build` script + a `prepack` so any `npm pack`/publish regenerates dist) compiles `tsconfig.build.json` and copies the drizzle migrations (`*.sql` + `meta/_journal.json`) next to the dist `migrations-runner.js`; the `files` allow-list ships `dist/**/*.sql` + `dist/infrastructure/db/migrations/meta/**` so `runMigrations` resolves the journal from the published tarball at runtime (a `/review` caught that an earlier draft copied them to dist but excluded them from the pack manifest — they are now shipped + guarded by a positive pack-manifest assertion). The two vitest configs alias every `@usetheo/memory` subpath back to its `src` entry, so the contract (930) + integration suites still exercise live source rather than a stale build. The `examples/chat-with-memory` demo dependency + `node_modules` symlink were repointed from the workspace root (the pre-reorg location of the package) to `packages/core`, where `@usetheo/memory` now lives. Verified end-to-end: the demo round-trips `remember` (LLM extraction via `gpt-4o-mini`) → 384-dim local-embedder → pgvector → `recall` against the published `dist` build (`/api/chat` + `/api/memory/recall`).
- **`memories_kind_derived_from_check` NULL hole (migration 0015)** — a `kind='derived'` row with `derived_from=NULL` was wrongly ADMITTED: the CHECK's derived branch `jsonb_array_length(derived_from) >= 1` evaluated to NULL (not FALSE) for a NULL array, and Postgres only rejects a CHECK when it is FALSE. The corrected branch requires `derived_from IS NOT NULL` explicitly, so a derived memory without provenance is now rejected as intended. New migration `0015_fix_kind_derived_from_check.sql` drops + re-adds the constraint; `schema.ts` + drizzle meta regenerated (the 3 hand-authored migrations 0011/0013/0014 also gained their previously-missing `meta/*_snapshot.json`, restoring drizzle journal↔snapshot parity).
- **`themory migrate` / dist migrations path (production)** — the build copied migration SQL + drizzle `meta/_journal.json` to `dist/infrastructure/db/migrations` (a pre-reorg path), but the dist `migrations-runner.js` now lives at `dist/core/src/infrastructure/db/` and resolves `./migrations` next to itself. The mismatch made the built/Dockerized CLI fail with `themory migrate: migrations failed (Can't find meta/_journal.json file)`. `postbuild:copy-migrations` now targets `dist/core/src/infrastructure/db/migrations`; its log moved to stderr so `npm pack --json` output stays parseable.
- **dist CLI optional-dep resolution (production)** — `@xenova/transformers` + `openai` are declared at the workspace-root `dependencies` so the root-resident `themory` bin (`dist/agent-tools/cli.js`) resolves them at runtime. Under pnpm they were only linked under `packages/core/node_modules`, so a `themory server`/`mcp`/verb boot from the repo root failed with `Cannot find package '@xenova/transformers'`.
- **dim-change canary read-outside-transaction** — `applyDimChange`'s post-ALTER value-based canary used `pool.query` (a separate pooled connection) to verify the new `vector(N)` dimensions, but the `ALTER`s run inside a `BEGIN/COMMIT` on a dedicated `client` connection. Under READ COMMITTED isolation the canary read the *pre-ALTER* catalog (old dim) and false-failed with `dim-change post-ALTER canary FAILED: entities.embedding atttypmod=1536 ≠ targetDim=384`, rolling back every parametric dim change. This broke ALL non-default `embeddingDim` migrations (e.g. the 384-dim local-embedder path) — `runMigrations(pool, { embeddingDim })` always threw. Fix: the canary now reads on `client` (inside the transaction), observing the uncommitted ALTER it is meant to verify. Regression coverage: `tests/integration/migrations/dim-change.test.ts` (11/11 green) + `tests/integration/adapters-or-local-e2e-smoke.test.ts` (real-LLM 384-dim write+recall round-trip green). Also fixed 3 stale assertions in the adapters E2E smoke (non-existent `new ManualReflectionPolicy` → `{ kind: 'manual' }` literal; `remember` returns `MemoryItem[]` not `{ facts }`), previously masked because the canary blocked `beforeAll`.
- **extract-cli-to-packages** — `themory` CLI now boots under plain Node after build (closes the plan's `themory --help após build` goal criterion, broken by the workspace reorg). Two defects: (1) `tsc`-emitted bare `@usetheo/*` specifiers in `dist/**` resolved through the workspace symlink back to TypeScript source (`ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND: packages/mcp/src/server.js`) — fixed by a `postbuild:rewrite-aliases` step (`scripts/rewrite-dist-aliases.mjs`) rewriting them to relative dist paths; (2) the CLI router statically imported the optional-dependency-backed `LocalTransformerEmbedder` (@xenova/transformers) + `OpenAILLM` (openai), so `themory --help` required those optional deps installed — fixed by lazy-importing them at the single construction site (`wireMemoryFromPool`). Also removed the vestigial broken `themory-mcp` bin from the private `@usetheo/memory-mcp` package (root package already provides it). Regression guard: `tests/integration/p0-audit/cli-dist-boot-envfree.test.ts`.
- **code-quality D2 workspace-awareness** ([ADR-0016](.claude/knowledge-base/adrs/ADR-0016-code-quality-d2-workspace-awareness.md)) — the `/code-quality` symbol-fabrication detector now recognizes all local pnpm/npm workspace package names as first-party, so sibling workspace imports (`@usetheo/memory-api`, `@usetheo/memory-mcp`, `@usetheo/memory/*` subpaths) are no longer false-flagged as fabricated npm packages. Closes a `FAIL_HARD` false positive (provably wrong: green typecheck + 930 passing tests import them) that blocked `/review` of the workspace reorg. Stable identifier unchanged; no enforcement softened. Regression guard: `test_typescript_symbol_fab_skips_pnpm_workspace_packages`.
- **code-review-fixes-v0-2-1** — 15 fixes from FAANG-style code review: defense-in-depth guard on `applyDimChange` DDL (CWE-89), rate limiter SHA-256 token hashing (CWE-200), streamable HTTP error sanitization (CWE-209), LLM network vs parse error differentiation in extraction pipeline, zero-dim vector OTel event, history persist failure logging, admin-keys `pool.connect` transaction safety (ADR D1/EC-3), OIDC cache LRU eviction, atomic `bumpReflectionCounter` upsert (ADR D5), rate limiter bucket eviction, reflection policies re-exported from root barrel, bearer auth verifier hoisted to construction time, 3 new OTel events (`extraction_parse_failure`, `embed_zero_dim_skipped`, `history_persist_failed`)

## [0.1.0] - 2026-06-09

### Added

- **themory-category-filter-server-side** — operator-supplied `category` field shipped end-to-end across the public write/list contract (RED TEAM F3 unblock from 2026-06-09 `theo-cloud/theo` commit `a3538930`). New optional `RememberOptions.category` (1-64 chars per ADR D3) routes through the extraction pipeline into `payload['category']` (T3.3) and is hoisted to a first-class top-level `category` column on the `memories` table (migration `0014_add_category_column.sql` + btree index `memories_category_idx` per ADR D2). New optional `GetAllOptions.category` filter at `LocalScopedMemory.getAll`; REST `GET /v1/scopes/{type}/{id}/memories?category=X` zod-validates the param (T4.1) and threads it through `applySkillFilters` → `Filters.category` → `composeFilters` predicate `category = $N`. New optional `MemoryItem.category` on the public DTO (EC-12 dual-pathway projection: ALSO available as `metadata.category` for upstream-shape parity, mirroring v1.1 attributed_to symmetry). New REST `RememberBody.category` mirrors the SDK. **ADR D1 honest disclosure**: category is operator-supplied — the LLM extraction pipeline does NOT auto-derive it; pre-2026-06-09 rows have `category=NULL` permanently and are NOT findable via the filter (per ADR D1 no-backfill + EC-9 NULL-findability JSDoc). **ADR D6 immutability**: UPSERT does NOT overwrite category — first-write wins per ADR-0003 ADD-only spirit; mutation requires `forget(id) + remember(newText, { category: 'B' })`. **EC-2 SDK boundary**: `runExtractionPipeline` throws `InvalidScope` at entry when `category.length === 0` OR `> 64` (defense-in-depth alongside REST zod min(1).max(64)). **EC-3 empty-string normalization**: 3-layer normalization (in-memory store, pgvector `composeFilters`, `LocalScopedMemory.getAll`) treats `filters.category === ''` as no-filter. **EC-4 / EC-5**: byte-equality case-sensitive + no Unicode normalization (NFC ≠ NFD). **EC-6**: parameterized binding rejects SQL-injection probes. Cross-repo BFF Go forwarding shipped at `theo-cloud/theo` commit `627f4ef9`; Dashboard test pin shipped at `theo-cloud/theo` commit `1cdc6005`. THE GOAL TEST: 8 RED→GREEN integration tests at `tests/integration/server/scopes-list-category-filter.test.ts` + 2 smoke gate at `tests/integration/server/category-filter-public-api-smoke.test.ts` + 10 RED→GREEN pgvector adapter at `tests/integration/pgvector-store-category.test.ts` + 4 migration idempotency tests + 6 unit tests at in-memory store + 5 unit tests at pipeline boundary, all against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`. PRIORITY 0 hardening of sub-goal 2 (SDK functional) per CLAUDE.md — dashboard category input was wired client-side since v1.5 but the SDK contract was missing.
- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle** (FAANG-grade auth lifecycle hardening per CLAUDE.md PRIORITY 0 sub-goals 2+3) — 3 layers shipped for `@usetheo/memory/server`: Camada 2 (OSS multi-user lifecycle: `theo_api_keys` migration `0012` + `ApiKeyAuthVerifier` 12th DIP impl + 5 admin REST handlers + `requireScope` middleware + bootstrap self-revoke + `themory keys {create,list,rotate,revoke}` CLI), Camada 3 (OIDC bridge: `OidcAuthVerifier` RFC 7662 introspection with EC-4/EC-5/EC-12 absorptions + `dualValidationAuth` Hono middleware with EC-1 fallthrough), MCP HTTP sub-goal 3 (`withBearerAuth` node:http wrapper + `connectStreamableHttp({bearerAuth})` + ADR-0015 #3 non-localhost TLS guard + `themory mcp --auth=<mode>` flag), RFC 9728 well-known endpoint, and 3 new ADRs (ADR-0013/0014/0015 + ADR-0009 supersession footer). THE GOAL TEST: 12/12 integration scenarios GREEN against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`.
- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle-fixes-cycle-1** — closes 2 honest deferrals from parent cycle: (a) admin REST `/v1/admin/keys/*` now mountable via opt-in `createServer({ adminApi: { pool, bootstrapToken? } })` per ADR D1; (b) production `oauth4webapi`-backed `IntrospectFn` adapter at `src/server/auth/oidc-introspect-oauth4webapi.ts` exposing `createOauth4webapiIntrospect({ issuer, clientId, clientSecret })` (ADR D2+D3 — discovery cached at construction per RFC 8414). New CLI flags `--issuer <URL>` + `--client-id <id>` on BOTH `themory server` AND `themory mcp` (env equivalents: `THEMORY_OIDC_ISSUER`, `THEMORY_OIDC_CLIENT_ID`). Secrets env-only per ADR D4: `THEMORY_OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET` (NO `--client-secret` flag — `ps` leak risk). THE GOAL TEST: 6/6 e2e scenarios + 12/12 lifecycle sentinel GREEN.
- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle-fixes-cycle-1** — public surface re-exports from `@usetheo/memory/server` (F-dom-api-1 absorption from `/review`): `ApiKeyAuthVerifier`, `AuthPool`, `requireScope`, `RequireScopeOptions`, `isBootstrapAllowed`, `markBootstrapConsumedTx`, `OidcAuthVerifier`, `IntrospectFn`, `IntrospectionResult`, `OidcAuthVerifierOptions`, `dualValidationAuth`, `DualValidationOptions`, `createOauth4webapiIntrospect`, `CreateOauth4webapiIntrospectOptions`, `wellKnownOauthProtectedResourceHandler`, `OidcDiscoveryConfig`. Third-party consumers can now compose admin REST + OIDC + dual-validation without internal subpath imports.
- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle-fixes-cycle-1** — `IntrospectionResult` expanded from 4 to 12 standard RFC 7662 § 2.2 claims (F-dom-api-3 absorption from `/review`): added `client_id`, `username`, `token_type`, `iat`, `nbf`, `aud`, `iss`, `jti`. The `aud` claim is load-bearing for audience-validation per RFC 8707 § 1 — consumers MAY enforce aud-check against their canonical resource URL to prevent token-confusion attacks.
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2** — `wireMemoryFromPool(pool)` shared Memory factory helper at `@usetheo/memory/agent-tools` (M1 closure; consolidates the `PgvectorStore` + `LocalTransformerEmbedder` + `OpenAILLM` tuple across 3 callers).
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2** — 8 NEW RED tests at `tests/integration/cli/verbs-cli-fixes-cycle-2.test.ts` locking the strict-coercion contract (4) + dist hygiene (1) + regex-pinned sentinel (1) + EC-1 empty-string + EC-2 hex notation (2).
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2** — `npm run prebuild:clean` script chained before `tsc` in `build` (M5 closure) + `prepack` auto-build (EC-5 absorption).
- **MCP `remember` schema gains optional `infer?: boolean` flag (issue /tmp/themory-issue.md #2)** — `src/agent-tools/tools/remember-schema.ts` `INPUT_SCHEMA` now exposes `infer` (default `true` — preserves existing extraction behavior). When `false`, the handler passes through to `ScopedMemory.remember(..., { infer: false })` and the content is stored verbatim, mirroring `POST /v1/remember`'s `infer` flag. Closes the REST↔MCP asymmetry that blocked literal-write use cases (caller-supplied facts, already-formatted tool outputs) over MCP.


### Changed

- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (ADR D1 — M4 closure)** — `parseIntInRange` + `parseFloatInRange` factories at `src/agent-tools/cli.ts` adopt STRICT coercion via `Number(raw)` + regex pre-check (`/^-?\d+$/` for ints, `/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/` for floats) + isInteger/isFinite + range. Replaces fix-cycle-1's lossy `Number.parseInt`/`Number.parseFloat`.
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (ADR D3 — M8 closure)** — `MemoryConfig.reflectionPolicy` type widened from `?: ReflectionPolicy` to `?: ReflectionPolicy | undefined` so the ADR-0005 v1.1 explicit-`undefined` opt-in is typed-reachable under `exactOptionalPropertyTypes: true`. Cascades to `CloudMemoryConfig extends MemoryConfig` (cloud pre-release; no runtime impact).
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (T2.1 — M2 closure)** — `MemoryConfig.reflectionPolicy` JSDoc extended with 3-path description (omitted/explicit-undefined/explicit-policy) + `@see ADR-0005 § Supplementary amendment v1.1`.
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (T3.1 — M1 closure)** — `createAgentToolsMemory` refactored to call `wireMemoryFromPool(pool)`; `run-mcp-server.ts::runMcpServer` and `run-server.ts::runServer` (inside EC-33 warm Promise) likewise delegate to the shared helper. Single source of truth for the provider tuple across CLI/MCP/REST.
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (T3.3 — M6 + EC-4 closure)** — Locked-names sentinels at `src/__tests__/cli-locked-names.test.ts` + `tests/integration/cli/verbs-cli-fixes.test.ts` tests 5+6 now use REGEX-PINNED matches against `.command(...)`/`.option(...)` call sites with boundary char `[\s'"<]` (rejects `--user-name`-style compound options + factory-arg contexts).
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (T5.1 — M10 closure)** — Re-categorized fix-cycle-1 entries below per Keep-a-Changelog discipline (was a single mega-bullet under `### Added`).
- **themory-mcp-bearer-by-env-faang — env-driven MCP transport default (T2.1 + T2.2 + T3.1 + T4.1)** — `themory mcp` auto-selects `--transport=streamable-http --auth=api-keys` when `THEMEMORY_API_KEY` env is present AND no `--transport`/`--auth` flag (nor `THEOMEM_MCP_TRANSPORT`/`THEOMEM_MCP_AUTH` env) was explicit. 3-tier precedence (per ADR D1): CLI flag > scoped env > `THEMEMORY_API_KEY` presence (non-empty after `.trim` per EC-1) > stdio fallback. Auth mode mirrors the same precedence; Tier-3 auto-selects `api-keys` ONLY when transport is `streamable-http` (ADR D2 — no silent security downgrade). New `@internal` `resolveMcpModes` composer (EC-2 single-export discipline; individual resolvers stay module-private; barrel `src/agent-tools/index.ts` unchanged). New stderr disclosure announces the auto-selected pair on startup (ADR D3 Princípio 3 auditability); suppressible via `THEOMEM_MCP_QUIET=1` (EC-6 embedded-use escape). 14-cell precedence matrix locked at `src/agent-tools/__tests__/run-mcp-server.test.ts`. Consumer DX: one `theo_live_*` bearer token now authenticates uniformly across REST (`@usetheo/memory/server` Item 16) + MCP HTTP (no consumer needs to remember which surface accepts which credential). Backward compat: `RunMcpServerOptions` shape unchanged; stdio default preserved when env absent; explicit flags always win (Tier-1). PRIORITY 0 hardening of sub-goal 3 (MCP available + reliable) per CLAUDE.md § PRIORITY 0 — uses Item 20 `ApiKeyAuthVerifier` shipped 2026-06-07 as-is; zero new deps; zero public API surface added. README § Agent loops via MCP gains "Uniform Bearer auth across REST + MCP HTTP (FAANG-DX)" subsection documenting the path + R4 warning against unintentional `.mcp.json` auto-flip.


### Removed

- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (ADR D5 — M3 closure)** — `createCliMemory` backward-compat alias deleted from `agent-tools-memory.ts`. The 4 verb handlers (`run-{remember,recall,forget,reflect}.ts`) migrated to import `createAgentToolsMemory` directly. The alias had zero external consumers (never in `package.json#exports` barrel); hard delete per Inquebrável Rule 11 (YAGNI).


### Fixed

- **MCP `remember` data-loss honesty (issue /tmp/themory-issue.md #1)** — `rememberHandler` at `src/agent-tools/tools/remember-handler.ts` no longer discards the return value of `ScopedMemory.remember`. Previously every call returned `"Memory persisted."` with `isError:false` regardless of outcome, making LLM-extraction failures (drafts=[] OR provider 401/timeout/parse error swallowed by the catch at `src/core/extraction/pipeline.ts:299-302`) invisible to MCP callers — silent data-loss. Handler now propagates actual item count + ids when persisted, and returns `isError:true` with explicit "no facts extracted; nothing was persisted" message pointing operators at OTel events `extraction_failed` / `extraction_zero_facts` for diagnostics. Refactoring the pipeline catch to distinguish `LLM_UNAVAILABLE` vs `EXTRACTION_ZERO_FACTS` is the ideal next step but deferred (cross-caller architectural change — separate cycle).

- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle-fixes-cycle-1 (F-arch-2 absorption from /review)** — `themory server` + `themory mcp` no longer crash at boot when a stale `THEMORY_OIDC_ISSUER` env var is present but OIDC was NOT explicitly selected (e.g., `themory mcp --auth=api-keys`). Pre-fix: `resolveOidcConfig` fired unconditionally; any leftover env from a prior debug session would throw EC-5/EC-6. Now gated on `mode === 'oidc' || mode === 'both'` (MCP) AND on `--issuer` flag OR `THEMORY_OIDC_ISSUER` env being explicitly intended (REST server). Backward-compat sub-goal 5 of fixes-cycle-1 plan restored.
- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle-fixes-cycle-1 (F-arch-1 absorption from /review)** — `themory server` admin REST sub-app now mounts ONLY when `THEMORY_ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_TOKEN` env is set (the canonical opt-in per ADR D1+D4). Pre-fix: also auto-mounted when `THEMEMORY_API_KEY` was set, which violated ADR D1's "ONLY when adminApi supplied" contract. ADR D1 opt-in contract honored end-to-end.
- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle-fixes-cycle-1 (F-dom-api-2 absorption from /review)** — `requireScope` middleware now emits RFC 6750 § 3 `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer` header on 401 responses (was missing). 401 with no scheme indicator violates RFC 6750 and confuses OAuth-aware HTTP clients (e.g., `oauth4webapi`'s built-in challenge follower).
- **theo-memory-faang-auth-lifecycle-fixes-cycle-1 (F-dom-cli-8 absorption from /review)** — `resolveBootstrapToken` now returns the TRIMMED value when non-empty (was returning un-trimmed `raw`). Fixes a silent determinism divergence where env=`" abc "` would pass the non-empty check but the bearer comparison used `" abc "` vs operator-typed `"abc"` → no-match.
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (M4)** — silent numeric coercion in `parseIntInRange`/`parseFloatInRange` (`--top-k 5.7` → 5, `--top-k 5abc` → 5, `--threshold 0.5abc` → 0.5) across all 5 collapsed validators (--top-k, --threshold, --diversify, --port×2). Now exits 2 with `Invalid --flag: <raw> (must be ...)` per Inquebrável Rule 8 (fail-fast at boundary).
- **verbs-as-cli-fixes-cycle-2 (M5)** — stale `dist/agent-tools/cli-memory-factory.js` shipped in `npm pack` manifest after fix-cycle-1 git mv. `prebuild:clean` rmSync now guarantees fresh dist on every build; sentinel test 5 asserts file absent post-build.

### Added

- **principles-alignment-hardening T1.1 → T5.1 — close 4 honest gaps against CLAUDE.md § IMPORTANTE Princípios** — closes the 23% alignment gap surfaced by the 2026-06-04 audit against the 3 source-of-truth principles at the top of CLAUDE.md (`Princípio 1` confirmação factor, `Princípio 2` priorização > volume, `Princípio 3` IGNORE auditability). Public-API surface gains: (a) `MemoryItem.confirmationCount?: number` — Princípio 1 confirmação counter bumped on dedup-collision UPSERT (PgvectorStore composite ON CONFLICT per ADR D5); pre-cycle rows backfill to DEFAULT 1 (EC-4 honest floor disclosure). (b) `MemoryItem.rejectedAsDuplicate?: boolean` — Princípio 3 IGNORE auditability surface: `true` when remember returns the EXISTING row (hash + scope tuple already known), `false` (or undefined) for fresh inserts. (c) `RecallOptions.diversify?: { lambda: number; topPool?: number }` — Princípio 2 opt-in MMR rerank reducing near-paraphrase result-set redundancy. EC-3 absorption: `lambda ∉ [0, 1]` throws `InvalidScope` at boundary. Default (undefined) byte-identical to v1.5. (d) 3 new `MemoryEvent` literals over the existing `emitEvent` infra: `extraction_zero_facts` (LLM returned drafts=[]); `dedup_collision_confirmed` (composite UPSERT bumped confirmation_count instead of inserting); `recall_threshold_filtered` (sub-threshold rows dropped — fires only when filtered_count > 0). Total `MEMORY_EVENTS` count: 13 → 16. (e) `VectorStore.insert` DIP widened to return `Promise<readonly InsertOutcome[]>` exposing per-id UPSERT semantics to the wrap site so consumers SEE the rejection instead of receiving silent []. THE GOAL TEST `tests/integration/principles-alignment-hardening.test.ts` PASSES with 19 assertions verifying the 4 sub-goals end-to-end against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1` (Xenova MiniLM 384-dim). Includes cross-scope isolation (EC-1 absorption: alice + bob each get an independent row), 3 IGNORE events captured via InMemorySpanExporter, rejectedAsDuplicate flag propagation, and Princípio 2 diversify reduction measurement (EC-9 honest disclosure: weak invariant `diversified ≤ baseline` asserted; redundancy percentage emitted to stdout for honest review — NO silent fixture padding per Inquebrável Rule 3). ### Fixed

- **principles-alignment-hardening T0.1 — v0.1 cross-scope dedup defect** — migration 0011 replaces the v0.1 global UNIQUE on `memories.hash` with a scope-aware composite UNIQUE on `(hash, COALESCE(user_id,''), COALESCE(session_id,''), COALESCE(agent_id,''))` per ADR D5 (EC-1 absorption). Prior behavior: when `user:alice` wrote a fact and later `user:bob` wrote the same text, the global UNIQUE silently rejected bob's INSERT — bob received alice's memoryId, a cross-scope data leak violating the ADR-0002 three-scope contract. Fixed behavior: same-text writes from different scopes produce independent rows; each scope owns its own dedup signal. Backward-compatible at the row level (pre-cycle DBs had at most 1 row per hash by virtue of the v0.1 constraint, so the composite index installs cleanly). Also adds `confirmation_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1` column per ADR D1 — Princípio 1 (CLAUDE.md § IMPORTANTE) confirmação factor surfaces as a queryable signal. Pre-cycle rows backfill to DEFAULT 1 — EC-4 honest floor disclosure: the v0.1 schema did not track confirmations, so 1 is not fabricated history.

### Changed

- **semantic-recall-cache-v1-5-fixes-cycle-1 T3.1 — Document `repo` scope tier deferral per ADR D10** — `README.md § Semantic recall cache (v1.5)` gains a "Known limitation (v1.5.0)" callout disclosing that `memory.skills(repoId).recall(...)` does NOT consult the recall cache — the cache wrap lives on `LocalScopedMemory.recall` only. `ScopeTierTtlConfig.repo` field remains REQUIRED in the public DIP because consumer configs that set it forward-port without rewrites; the field's TTL value is dormant until the wiring lands. Skill-scope cache wiring is tracked for v1.5.x. Mirrors the v1.1 EC-9 skill-scope `asOf` honest deferral precedent. Closes F-merge-2 + F-merge-8 from `/review semantic-recall-cache-v1-5` (2026-06-03). @see `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/semantic-recall-cache-v1-5-fixes-cycle-1-plan.md § ADR D10 + § T3.1`.

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1 T2.3 — F-rev-3 absorption: ADR D3 amendment for health auth-exempt** — source plan `theo-memory-standalone-p0-plan.md` bumped v1.3 → v1.4 with ADR D3 amendment block documenting that the bearer middleware on `/v1/*` EXEMPTS `GET /v1/health` + `GET /v1/health/db` (mirroring the EC-19 pattern for `GET /v1/openapi.json`). Rationale: RFC 9110 § 5.1 + OWASP `/healthz` convention — orchestrator probes (Kubernetes readiness/liveness, ECS health checks, docker-compose `--wait`) do NOT carry credentials. Without this exemption, the docker-compose healthcheck round-trip in T3.5 GOAL TEST cannot complete (orchestrator gets 401 → container marked unhealthy → `--wait` times out). The behavior shipped in commit `fc4ec5c` (T3.5) but the ADR amendment was missing — cycle-implement anti-pattern #7 (silent scope creep). This entry closes the documentation gap. Regression pinned by `src/server/__tests__/handlers.test.ts` tests 11a + 11b asserting 200/503 (NOT 401) when `THEMEMORY_API_KEY` is set. Closes `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0` F-rev-3 MAJOR.

- **semantic-recall-cache-v1-5 T5.1 + T5.2 — THE GOAL TEST (hit rate 6/10 = 0.60 ≥ 0.50) + docs flip Roadmap Item 19 to Shipped** — **T5.1 (THE GOAL TEST)**: integration test `tests/integration/cache/semantic-recall-cache.test.ts` proves Item 19 Acceptance Criterion #1 against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`. Composition: `LocalTransformerEmbedder` (Xenova `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`, 384-dim, L2-normalized — ships in repo per v1.4 `adapters-openrouter-local-embedder`); `DeterministicStubLLM` (extraction quality is NOT the metric here — cache hit rate is); `PgvectorStore` + `runMigrations({ embeddingDim: 384 })`; `createLocalMemory({ recallCache: { threshold: 0.8 } })` opt-in cache wiring. Measurement: in-memory capturing MeterProvider counts `theo.memory.recall_cache.hit` Counter emissions across 10 sequential `(a, b)` pair runs (truncate cache between pairs to isolate). Fixture set at `tests/fixtures/cache/near-duplicate-query-pairs.ts` — 10 hand-authored English query pairs designed to test the 0.8 threshold realistically ("what is my favorite color?" vs "what color do I prefer?", "where do I live?" vs "what is my home address?", etc.). **Result: hits=6/10 → hit rate 0.60** (target ≥ 0.50). 4 pairs fall under the 0.8 threshold with the Xenova embedder — HONEST measurement per Inquebrável Rule 3 (no fixture rewording was needed to reach 0.5; the 0.60 is real signal). 3 sibling regression smokes in the same file per plan T5.1 Acceptance Criteria: (a) write-trigger invalidation — `remember` after pre-warm cache zeros the row count (Item 19 AC#3 inline regression guard); (b) TTL expiry — `ttlSecondsByScopeTier=1s` + `sleep(2s)` → MISS via no counter emission; (c) stub-embedder gate — `DeterministicStubEmbedder` inline-defined at 384-dim (the shipped `src/embedders/stub.ts` class is 1536-dim and would be rejected by the dim probe at 384-dim DB) + factory invocation captures 3-line stderr warning matching `/DeterministicStubEmbedder/`. **`applyDimChange` cascade extension**: now drops `recall_cache_scope_hnsw_idx`, deletes from `recall_cache` (if exists), ALTERs `recall_cache.query_embedding` TYPE, and recreates the HNSW index inside the SAME BEGIN/COMMIT transaction as `memories` + `entities` cascades. Cache rows are stale once embedder dim shifts (cosine across dimensions has no meaning); safe to DELETE — cache is read-side memoization, not authoritative state. Guarded by `IF EXISTS` check on `information_schema.tables` for pre-v1.5 DBs that lack the table. **T5.2 (docs)**: `README.md` gains "### Semantic recall cache (v1.5)" subsection in the DEEP DIVE technical area (per public-copy.md voice rules — internals belong in `## How it works` downward) with consumer example code, threshold/TTL/eviction explanation, write-trigger invalidation contract, ADR D8 bypass boundary (`asOf` + `attributedTo`), 3 OTel instrument names, `@usetheo/memory/cache-pg` subpath, stub-embedder gate behavior, and a citation back to THE GOAL TEST hit rate. `CLAUDE.md` Roadmap Item 19 status flipped from `Planned` → `Shipped 2026-06-03` with: (a) AC#1 hit rate LOCKED at 0.50 (was TBD), achieved 0.60 — honest disclosure of fixture choice + embedder behavior caveat per Inquebrável Rule 3; (b) blueprint Rec #9 amendment baked into the Item 19 description ("at the `recall` layer (embedchain caches RAG responses at a different layer)"); (c) **`/dogfood` gate REMAINS EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT** — v1.5 does NOT unblock production-ready claims by itself per dogfood-golden-rule.md; Roadmap Item 11 v1.0 gate still requires the anchor scenario running ≥ 30 days. Total Phase 5: 27 → 31 cache integration PASS (+4); contract suite unchanged at 997 PASS (no new contract tests required — sibling regressions live in integration since they need real pgvector + LocalTransformerEmbedder). - **semantic-recall-cache-v1-5 T2.3 + T3.1 + T4.1 — stub-embedder loud warning + 3 OTel metrics (Item 19 AC#2) + `@usetheo/memory/cache-pg` subpath export with bundle isolation** — **T2.3 (stub-embedder gate)**: when `createLocalMemory({ recallCache })` resolves an opt-in cache AND the configured embedder is a `StubEmbedder` OR `DeterministicStubEmbedder` (constructor.name match, avoids `instanceof` coupling), emit a 3-line stderr warning mirroring cycle-1 T2.2 / F-rev-9 precedent at `src/agent-tools/run-server.ts:110-118` (line 1 names the detected class; line 2 surfaces the behavior delta — "Cache hits will be COINCIDENTAL (deterministic but non-semantic vectors), NOT semantic"; line 3 points to README § Wiring real providers). Inquebrável Rule 3 (honesty) at the consumer-facing boundary. **EC-14 absorption (DOCUMENT)**: contract tests at `src/local/__tests__/embedder-cache-gate-warning.test.ts` install `vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write')` in `beforeEach` (mirror v0.6.1 / cycle-1 EC-6 pattern) so the warning is captured WITHOUT polluting the 997 baseline. **T3.1 (3 OTel instruments)**: new module `src/local/recall-cache/recall-cache-metrics.ts` exposes 3 lazy-init resolvers per F-dom-6 v0.4b-fixes T10.1 precedent (resolves Meter on FIRST observation so a `MeterProvider` registered AFTER `import @usetheo/memory` is honored — module-load resolution captures the no-op default provider permanently, the F-dom-6 bug avoided): `getRecallCacheHitCounter` → `theo.memory.recall_cache.hit` (Counter INT, cumulative HIT count); `getRecallCacheHitDurationHistogram` → `theo.memory.recall_cache.hit.duration_ms` (Histogram DOUBLE, HIT path latency observed at the cache `get` site); `getRecallCacheMissDurationHistogram` → `theo.memory.recall_cache.miss.duration_ms` (Histogram DOUBLE, MISS path latency observed at the `Memory.recall` wrap site — includes full 9-step retrieval + cache populate cost). Per blueprint D5 + Item 19 AC#2 label shape `{ scope_tier, embedder_kind }` (constructor.name of the configured Embedder, threaded via new `PgvectorRecallCacheStoreOptions.embedderKind?` field; defaults `'unknown'` when omitted). Hit-rate computable consumer-side as `count(hit_counter) / (count(hit_counter) + count(miss_histo))`. Test-only `__resetRecallCacheMetricsForTesting` clears the lazy cache so subsequent `getRecallCache*` calls re-resolve against the now-current MeterProvider. **T4.1 (subpath + bundle isolation)**: new subpath `@usetheo/memory/cache-pg` (mirror `@usetheo/memory/embedders/local` v1.4 precedent) at `src/local/recall-cache/index.ts` re-exports `PgvectorRecallCacheStore` + `PgvectorRecallCacheStoreOptions` + 3 metric resolvers + 13th-DIP types + 3 default constants + `validateRecallCacheStoreConfig`. `package.json#exports."./cache-pg"` declared. **EC-26 bundle isolation gate**: the v1.5 cache impl ENTERS the default `@usetheo/memory` bundle ONLY when the consumer opts in via `recallCache: { ... }` — `resolveRecallCacheStore` and `Memory.recall` miss-path use `await import('./recall-cache/*.js')` DYNAMIC imports so the static-import walk from `src/index.ts` does NOT reach `pgvector-recall-cache-store.ts` OR `recall-cache-metrics.ts`. The 13th-DIP types live in `src/core/dip/recall-cache-store.ts` and are part of the public contract barrel; runtime cost is paid only on the cache-wired path. **EC-52 (npm pack manifest)**: integration test asserts `npm pack --dry-run` ships `src/local/recall-cache/index.ts`, `pgvector-recall-cache-store.ts`, `recall-cache-metrics.ts` AND excludes `__tests__/**`. Test coverage net: **8/8 contract** (4 T2.3 stub-warning + 4 T3.1 lazy-init) + **8/8 integration** (3 T3.1 OTel metric observation via inline capturing MeterProvider — no new `@opentelemetry/sdk-metrics` dep per Inquebrável Rule 11 YAGNI; 5 T4.1 subpath isolation — default barrel does NOT reach the impl, cache-pg barrel re-export smoke import, 4 pack-manifest assertions). Total: 989 → 997 contract PASS (+8); 22 → 27 cache integration PASS (+5); subpath-isolation grows by 5 entries (31 → 36). Lint + typecheck clean. Phase 3 → Phase 5: T5.1 THE GOAL TEST (10 near-duplicate pairs, hit rate ≥ 0.5) + T5.2 README + CHANGELOG roadmap-Item-19 status flip follow.

- **semantic-recall-cache-v1-5 T2.1 + T2.2 — `LocalMemoryConfig.recallCache` factory wiring + write-trigger scope invalidation hooks (Item 19 AC#3)** — extends `createLocalMemory` to accept a dual-shape `recallCache?: RecallCacheStoreConfig | RecallCacheStore` field per blueprint D5; cache is OFF by default, opt-in only. **Discriminator** `isRecallCacheStoreInstance(value)` distinguishes pre-built DIP impls from POJO config (4-method shape check). **DIP-instance path** uses the impl verbatim (escape hatch — caller pre-built a Redis-backed store, or a test double). **POJO config path** validates via `validateRecallCacheStoreConfig` (T0.1) → constructs `PgvectorRecallCacheStore` against `config.vectorStore.poolForEviction`. **POJO config + non-Postgres vector store** throws `InvalidCacheConfig` (the default impl lives in Postgres; in-memory adapter has no matching surface). **`Memory.recall` cache wrap** at `LocalScopedMemory.recall`: when cache is wired AND `RecallOptions.asOf === undefined` AND `RecallOptions.attributedTo === undefined` (ADR D8 applicability boundary), embed query once → consult cache → on HIT return cached results spread as a fresh array → on MISS run the existing 9-step retrieval pipeline → `set` populate before returning. **EC-4 + EC-5 absorption**: `asOf` OR `attributedTo` set → full BYPASS (no get, no set) because the v1.5.0 cache key `(scope_key, query_embedding)` does NOT include these dimensions; serving cached current/unfiltered results when the consumer asked for a historical OR peer-filtered slice would be a correctness bug per Inquebrável Rule 3. **EC-2 absorption (mid-deployment dim mismatch)**: `probeCacheTableDim(pool)` reads `pg_attribute.atttypmod` for `recall_cache.query_embedding` and the factory rejects construction when the dim diverges from the effective embedder dim (mirrors v1.4 `adapters-openrouter-local-embedder` T2.1 `atttypmod` empirical correction — pgvector v0.5.1 stores N directly, NOT N+4); pre-migration null-table case is honored (returns null → factory accepts → consumer runs `runMigrations` afterward; subsequent set would surface the missing-table error loudly per Inquebrável Rule 8). **EC-11 absorption (write-recall race)**: `remember` / `forget` / `reflect` await `invalidateScope` SEQUENTIALLY before returning so the consumer's next `recall` in the same scope sees the fresh post-write state — no race window. **EC-10 absorption (forget scope derivation)**: `LocalMemory.forget` is top-level (not scope-bound); `deriveScopeFromPayload(existing.payload)` reads `user_id` / `session_id` / `agent_id` from the persisted memory row to issue a targeted `invalidateScope` instead of an all-scope sweep — the most specific tier wins (session > agent > user); when the row carries no recognizable tier (defensive — pre-v0.1 rows in theory), no invalidation is issued and the cache continues serving until TTL (bounded staleness). **EC-6 absorption (reflect-derived staleness)**: `reflect` produces derived memories (v0.4a) + distillation rows (v0.7) — without scope invalidation the cache serves stale results post-reflect for the same scope. Hook fires post-success only when `persisted.length > 0` (no invalidation if reflect was a no-op). **Loud-but-degrade error policy**: all 3 hook sites wrap `invalidateScope` in try/catch + `console.error` so cache backend failures do NOT poison `remember`/`forget`/`reflect` returns (cache failure is a degraded path — caller falls through to the 9-step pipeline on the next recall). **Backward compatibility**: when `config.recallCache === undefined`, both classes thread `recallCacheStore: undefined` so the existing recall path is byte-identical to v1.4 (verified by zero contract regression across 979 baseline → 989 net tests). Test coverage: **10/10 contract** (factory-recall-cache.test.ts — DIP-instance verbatim use; cache HIT short-circuit; EC-4 `asOf` BYPASS; EC-5 `attributedTo` BYPASS; EC-4+EC-5 combined BYPASS; POJO threshold=1.5 throws `InvalidCacheConfig`; POJO + InMemoryVectorStore throws; `remember` success invokes invalidateScope; `invalidateScope` failure does NOT poison `remember` return) + **4/4 integration** (factory-wiring.test.ts — POJO `{ threshold: 0.8 }` constructs PgvectorRecallCacheStore + second recall HIT vs first MISS via row-count probe; Item 19 AC#3 regression — pre-warm cache + remember + verify post-write cache row count = 0 + next recall returns FRESH state; EC-2 cache-table-dim ≠ embedder-dim → `InvalidCacheConfig` throw; EC-2 pre-migration null table does NOT throw). Net: 979 → 989 contract PASS (+10); 18 → 22 cache integration PASS (+4). Lint + typecheck clean. Phase 2 → Phase 3: T2.3 stub-embedder loud warning + T3.1 3 OTel metrics + T4.1 subpath export follow.

- **semantic-recall-cache-v1-5 T1.1 + T1.2 — `PgvectorRecallCacheStore` core impl + capacity-bounded LRU eviction (SERIALIZABLE tx)** — ships the default `RecallCacheStore` impl at `src/local/recall-cache/pgvector-recall-cache-store.ts` backing the 13th DIP (T0.1) against the `recall_cache` table (T0.2). 4 methods within ADR-0008 method-count cap: `get` (cosine ≥ effective threshold + non-expired); `set` (write-through with LRU eviction inside SERIALIZABLE tx per EC-7); `invalidateScope` (DELETE all scope rows — write-trigger correctness gate); `clearExpired` (TTL sweep). Cache key derivation `deriveScopeKey(scope) → '${kind}:${id}'` at `src/local/recall-cache/scope-key.ts` (pure function, exhaustive switch over 4 Scope kinds including `repo` per ADR-0010). SERIALIZABLE tx wrapper at `src/local/recall-cache/serializable-tx.ts` retries once on `40001` (PG serialization_failure) per Postgres docs — bounded retry defends against connection pool amplification during incidents. **EC-7 absorption (LRU race)**: `Promise.all([set, set, set])` on a full scope serializes via PG SSI → final count = `maxSizePerScope` exactly (no over-delete, no over-insert; verified empirically with cap=5 + 3 concurrent inserts). **EC-8 absorption (PG NaN gap)**: PostgreSQL float comparisons treat NaN as GREATER-THAN all non-NaN values (per PG docs § 8.1.4) — opposite of IEEE 754 short-circuit. The plan's original claim was wrong for PG. Application-level guard at `get` entry short-circuits MISS when query embedding is zero-norm (cheap O(N) scan, N ≤ 1536) BEFORE the DB roundtrip; defensive comment in JSDoc + `isZeroNorm` helper. **EC-12 DOCUMENT absorption**: JSONB rows up to ~1 MB are routine in PG; typical `recall topK=100 × 4 KB/item ≈ 400 KB`; integration test exercises 500 KB JSONB write (10 items × 50 KB content) without crashing — boundary documented in class JSDoc. Module hygiene: 207 LoC (≤ 300 plan target; ≤ 500 architecture.md budget); 4 methods + 2 helpers + 5 SQL constants; cyclomatic ≤ 4 per method. SQL fully parameterized via `pg`'s `$N::vector` cast (security hygiene per architecture.md § 8 — no string interpolation of user input). Test coverage: **13/13 unit RED→GREEN** (mocked Pool — SQL composition, scope isolation, SERIALIZABLE BEGIN/COMMIT sequence, capacity-check DELETE-before-INSERT ordering, 40001 retry semantics, non-40001 no-retry); **14/14 integration PASS** against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1` (cosine HIT at 0.85 ≥ 0.8; cosine MISS at 0.70 < 0.8; TTL expiry; scope isolation; clearExpired sweep; zero-norm guard; large JSONB rows; LRU eviction at cap; eviction-respects-scope; EC-7 concurrent race). Total: 977 → 979 contract PASS + 18 integration PASS (4 from T0.2 + 14 from T1.1/T1.2). Phase 1 → Phase 2: T2.1 `LocalMemoryConfig.recallCache` factory wiring follows.

- **semantic-recall-cache-v1-5 T0.2 — Drizzle migration `0010_recall_cache.sql` + `recallCache` schema declaration + parity sentinel** — ships the `recall_cache` table backing the 13th DIP (T0.1). Columns: `id serial PK`, `scope_key text NOT NULL`, `query_embedding vector(1536) NOT NULL`, `result_set jsonb NOT NULL`, `hit_count int DEFAULT 0`, `created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now`, `expires_at timestamptz` (NULLABLE for non-TTL modes per blueprint D2), `threshold_used real DEFAULT 0.8`. Three indexes per blueprint § 3: HNSW `recall_cache_scope_hnsw_idx` (`USING hnsw (query_embedding vector_cosine_ops)`) powers cosine lookup at `get`; B-tree `recall_cache_scope_key_idx` powers `invalidateScope` DELETE perf; partial B-tree `recall_cache_expires_at_idx WHERE expires_at IS NOT NULL` powers `clearExpired` sweep. Idempotency via `CREATE TABLE/INDEX IF NOT EXISTS` mirrors v1.1 EC-6 pattern (closes orphan-schema collision per `architecture.md § 10.2`). Independent table — no FK back to memories; write-triggered invalidation via `invalidateScope` (T2.2) is the correctness gate. **EC-15 DOCUMENT absorption**: HNSW parameters `m=16, ef_construction=64` are conservative pgvector v0.5.1 defaults; tuning deferred to v1.5.x dogfood-evidence-driven adjustment per Inquebrável Rule 11 (YAGNI). Schema declaration `recallCache` at `src/local/schema.ts` matches SQL exactly; integration test `tests/integration/cache/migration-0010.test.ts` (4 tests) asserts: (1) migration applies to fresh `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1` and table is empty; (2) idempotent re-run is no-op; (3) all 3 indexes present with correct `USING hnsw`/`btree` strategy + partial-index `WHERE` clause; (4) **parity sentinel** — column types + nullability + `atttypmod=1536` match Drizzle declaration (v1.1 T0.1 precedent closing F-dom-dbmig-1 class of bug). Drizzle journal entry `0010_recall_cache` (idx=10, version=7) + `0010_snapshot.json` generated via `drizzle-kit generate`. `postbuild:copy-migrations` verified: `dist/local/migrations/0010_recall_cache.sql` ships in published bundle. Parametric dim discipline: column declares `vector(1536)`; T2.1 `applyDimChange` extension (next iteration) cascades dim changes to keep cache aligned with embedder per EC-2 `atttypmod` mismatch detection. Total: 966 contract PASS + 4 integration PASS (T0.2 net). Phase 0 → Phase 1: T1.1 `PgvectorRecallCacheStore` core impl follows.

- **semantic-recall-cache-v1-5 T0.1 — 13th DIP `RecallCacheStore` + `InvalidCacheConfig` typed error + 4-tier `ScopeTierTtlConfig`** — declares the read-side semantic-cache contract per Roadmap Item 19. Interface ships 4 methods (`get` / `set` / `invalidateScope` / `clearExpired`) within ADR-0008 method-count cap. `RecallCacheStoreConfig` carries `threshold` (default 0.8 — convergent across embedchain `similarity_threshold=0.8` AND `distance_threshold=0.2`), `ttlSecondsByScopeTier` (4 tiers: user 24h / agent 1h / session 10min / `repo` 1h — `repo` is skill-scope per ADR-0010), and `maxSizePerScope` (default 1000 mirroring embedchain `gptcache_data_manager(max_size=1000)`). `validateRecallCacheStoreConfig` enforces invariants at factory time per `architecture.md § 8` fail-fast: throws `InvalidCacheConfig` on out-of-range threshold, non-positive-integer `maxSizePerScope`, **NaN/Infinity numeric values (EC-1 absorption — IEEE 754 NaN comparison gap; `NaN < 0` and `NaN > 1` BOTH return false without `Number.isFinite` guard)**, or negative per-tier TTL. Pure types — ZERO runtime impl in `src/core/` per DIP boundary discipline; default impl `PgvectorRecallCacheStore` ships at T1.1 in `src/local/recall-cache/`. Public barrel `src/index.ts → src/core/index.ts` re-exports 4 types + 4 values (validator + 3 constants) + `InvalidCacheConfig`. Test coverage: 10/10 RED→GREEN tests including 6 EC absorption boundary tests. 956 → 966 baseline (+10). Consumes blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/semantic-recall-cache-blueprint.md` v1.0 (89/100 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS) + plan v1.1 (87.6/100 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS). Phase 0 of 5 — Phase 1 (PgvectorRecallCacheStore impl + LRU eviction) follows.

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1 T2.2 — F-rev-9 absorption: stub-embedder loud startup warning** — `themory server` AND `themory mcp` emit a 3-line stderr warning at startup banner the consumer-honesty gap: `DeterministicStubEmbedder` (REST) / `StubEmbedder` (MCP) + `StubLLM` produce deterministic but uncorrelated vectors — recall returns shape-correct results but only round-trips reliably on exact-text queries (no semantic matching). Inquebrável Rule 3 (honesty) at the consumer-facing boundary. EC-6 absorption: `beforeEach` `vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write').mockImplementation( => true)` shared infra in `run-server.test.ts` silences the warning across the 8 existing tests while the dedicated `FC1-T2.2-RED` test asserts the warning fires via the captured spy. EC-8 DOCUMENT: inline `// FOLLOW-UP: gate this warning when real-provider config lands` comments near the emit sites in both `run-server.ts` and `run-mcp-server.ts`. README "REST API server" gains "Wiring real providers (alpha default uses stubs)" sub-section explaining the disclosure + pointer to `OpenAIEmbedder` / `LocalTransformerEmbedder` consumer wiring (CLI `--embedder` flag is a future cycle). Closes `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0` F-rev-9 MAJOR.

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1 T2.1 — F-rev-4 absorption: 1MB body-limit DOS guard with structured 413 envelope** — `hono/body-limit` middleware mounted on `/v1/*` between OTel and bearer middlewares (order load-bearing per ADR D3 v1.1: `OTel → bodyLimit → bearer` so the DOS guard rejects oversized payloads BEFORE auth status is consulted — minor information-leak defense). EC-1 absorption: the `onError` callback explicitly constructs the `ContentTooLargeError` envelope via `mapDomainErrorToResponse` so 413 carries the same structured `{code: 'CONTENT_TOO_LARGE', message: ...}` body as every other 4xx response (`hono/body-limit`'s default 413 is plain text and would break consumers parsing 4xx envelopes uniformly per T1.6 contract). EC-7 absorption: middleware-order regression test asserts `2MB body + no Authorization + key set → 413 (NOT 401)`. EC-9 DOCUMENT: README "REST API server" section gains a note that Hono does NOT auto-decompress gzip request bodies; operators serving large compressed payloads enforce body limits at the reverse-proxy layer (nginx `client_max_body_size`, Caddy `request_body max_size`). 1MB cap chosen as generous for single-fact remember calls (typical body < 4 KB) with headroom for `infer:false` callers shipping pre-extracted fact arrays. Closes `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0` F-rev-4 MAJOR.

### Fixed

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-2 T1.4 — F-arch-2 + F-dom-api-6 absorption: ScopeTypeEnum description documents skill→501 semantic** — `src/server/openapi-registration.ts:24` declared `ScopeTypeEnum = z.enum(['user', 'session', 'agent', 'skill'])` WITHOUT a description, so consumers reading the spec saw all 4 path-param values as semantically equivalent — but `skill` returns 501 not 200 (per fixes-cycle-2 T1.2 / F-dom-api-1). Codegen SDK clients would emit guaranteed-501 methods with no honest contract. Fixed: applied `.describe(...)` directly on the `ScopeTypeEnum` zod schema with text documenting that `'skill'` returns 501 `SKILL_LISTING_NOT_IMPLEMENTED` and listing is SDK-only via `Memory.skills(...)` per ADR-0010. Single edit closes both F-arch-2 AND F-dom-api-6 (one root, two reviewers — D2 alternative B "split into two enums" rejected per YAGNI). `openapi.test.ts` test #8 (FC2-T1.4-RED) walks `spec.paths['/v1/scopes/{type}/{id}/memories'].get.parameters` finding the `type` param. EC-1 MUST FIX absorption: test #8 uses fallback chain `typeParam?.description ?? typeParam?.schema?.description` to handle BOTH OpenAPI 3.1-conformant placements (Parameter Object level vs Schema Object level) — verified empirically pre-implementation that NO existing `.describe` usage in `src/server/` existed to validate which path `@asteasolutions/zod-to-openapi` propagates description into. Both paths satisfy the F-arch-2 / F-dom-api-6 honesty intent (codegen consumers see the description regardless of nesting). EC-3 absorption: existing test #1 swagger-parser OpenAPI 3.1 validation still PASSES (Schema Object `description` is spec-conformant). Closes cycle-1-introduced F-arch-2 + F-dom-api-6 from `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1`.

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-2 T1.3 — F-dom-api-2 absorption: OpenAPI 413 CONTENT_TOO_LARGE for /v1/{remember,recall,forget,reflect}** — `src/server/app.ts:96-107` mounts `hono/body-limit` with `onError` returning a 413 envelope via `mapDomainErrorToResponse(new ContentTooLargeError(...))` (shipped fixes-cycle-1 T2.1 / F-rev-4). OpenAPI spec advertised only `201/202/204/400/422` (+500 on remember) — 413 undocumented. Same blast radius as F-dom-api-1: codegen SDK consumers + Pact contract tests would see an unannounced response code. Fixed: added identical 413 response entry to all 4 verb `registerPath` calls in `src/server/openapi-registration.ts` with `ErrorEnvelopeSchema` content; description cites T2.1 + F-rev-4 + `mapDomainErrorToResponse` inline (per `.claude/rules/no-paper-names-in-code.md` D4). `openapi.test.ts` test #7 (FC2-T1.3-RED) walks all 4 verbs asserting `responses['413']` defined + description matches `/1\s*MB|body|content too large/i`. EC-3 absorption: existing test #1 swagger-parser OpenAPI 3.1 validation still PASSES across all 4 verbs (4 new entries with identical description text accepted; spec remains structurally valid). Closes cycle-1-introduced F-dom-api-2 MAJOR from `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1`.

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-2 T1.2 — F-dom-api-1 absorption: OpenAPI 501 SKILL_LISTING_NOT_IMPLEMENTED for listScopeMemories** — `GET /v1/scopes/{type}/{id}/memories` runtime short-circuits `scope.type === 'skill'` to 501 `SKILL_LISTING_NOT_IMPLEMENTED` (per fixes-cycle-1 T1.1) but the OpenAPI spec at `src/server/openapi-registration.ts` advertised only `200/400/404/422`. Consumers using codegen (`openapi-generator`, `openapi-fetch`, `oazapfts`) or contract testing (Pact) would emit typed SDK methods that surface an undocumented status code. Fixed: added 501 response entry to the `listScopeMemories` `registerPath` call with `ErrorEnvelopeSchema` content; description cites ADR-0010 + F-rev-1 inline so codegen consumers see the breadcrumb (per `.claude/rules/no-paper-names-in-code.md` D4 — task ID / finding ID / ADR coordinate, no paper names). `openapi.test.ts` test #6 (FC2-T1.2-RED) walks `spec.paths['/v1/scopes/{type}/{id}/memories'].get.responses['501']` asserting presence + description match. EC-3 absorption: existing test #1 swagger-parser OpenAPI 3.1 validation still PASSES (spec remains structurally valid). Closes cycle-1-introduced F-dom-api-1 MAJOR from `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1`.

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-2 T1.1 — F-arch-7 = F-wire-1 absorption: MCP-side stub-embedder warning RED test** — new `src/agent-tools/__tests__/run-mcp-server.test.ts` pins the 3-line stderr stub-embedder warning emitted by `themory mcp` at `src/agent-tools/run-mcp-server.ts:91-103` (warning shipped in fixes-cycle-1 T2.2 / F-rev-9). Mirror of the REST-side `FC1-T2.2-RED` pattern in `run-server.test.ts`. EC-2 absorption: `beforeEach` stubs BOTH `THEOMEM_PG_URI` AND `THEOMEM_MCP_TRANSPORT` (empty string) so a system-env `THEOMEM_MCP_TRANSPORT=streamable-http` cannot route to the mock-uncovered streamable-http path. EC-6 absorption: file-level JSDoc cites the FOLLOW-UP coupling at `run-mcp-server.ts:93-94` so a future test-updater grepping for `FOLLOW-UP at run-mcp-server.ts:93-94` lands on this test when the conditional-warning gating sequel ships. Without this test, a future refactor could silently drop the warning lines and the CI gate would NOT catch it — closes the cycle-1-introduced F-arch-7 = F-wire-1 MAJOR finding from `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1`. Manual-RED discipline preserved per Inquebrável Rule 5 (literal-sense departure documented in commit message).

- **theo-memory-standalone-p0-fixes-cycle-1 T1.1 — F-rev-1 CRITICAL absorption** — `skillRepoId` removed from REST 4-verb scope keys + `GET /v1/scopes/skill/:id/memories` returns deterministic 501 `SKILL_LISTING_NOT_IMPLEMENTED`. Root cause: REST schemas at `src/server/schemas.ts:14` admitted `skillRepoId` as a 4th scope tier on `RememberBody`/`RecallBody`/`ReflectBody`; `resolveScope` routed it to `Memory.skills(repoId)` returning `ScopedSkillMemory` (`add`/`recall`/`consolidate`/`forget`); handlers then called `.remember`/`.reflect`/`.recall(query, options)` — methods that do NOT exist on `ScopedSkillMemory`. The `as unknown as MemoryLike` double-cast at `src/server/handlers/{remember,recall,reflect}.ts` bypassed TypeScript at the load-bearing call site. Production blast radius: 500 INTERNAL_ERROR on `POST /v1/{remember,recall,reflect}` with `skillRepoId` + `GET /v1/scopes/skill/:id/memories` (handler crashed in `pathParamsToScoped` calling `getAll` on `ScopedSkillMemory`). Test mocks shimmed `skills` with a fake `ScopedMemory` shape, masking the bug. Fix: removed `skillRepoId` from `SCOPE_KEYS`/`ScopeFields`/`resolveScope`/`MemoryLike` per CLAUDE.md ADR-0010 (skill scope is "exposed ONLY through `Memory.skills(...)` (not on the public 4 verbs)"); `scopesHandler` short-circuits the `'skill'` branch with 501 BEFORE the dispatch. 3 RED tests in `handlers.test.ts` + rewritten `scopes.test.ts` test #12 + new EC-4 short-circuit test + new EC-5 OpenAPI field-membership test. `as unknown as MemoryLike` double-cast dropped from all 3 handlers; `MemoryLike` type collapses to identity once `skills` member removed (also closes F-arch-2). Plan T1.1 + EC-2 + EC-4 + EC-5 absorbed. Closes `/review theo-memory-standalone-p0` F-rev-1 CRITICAL.

### Added

- **v0.1.0-alpha P0 Phase 3 — Docker stack (distroless image + production compose + GHCR workflow) — Roadmap Item 17** — ships the standalone deployment surface for `@usetheo/memory`. Multi-stage `Dockerfile` (T3.1) builds in three stages: (1) `builder` node:22-bookworm-slim runs `npm ci` + `npm run build` (tsc gate per EC-40 aborts publish if types fail); (2) `production-deps` node:22-bookworm-slim runs `npm ci --omit=dev --omit=optional` by default to keep the image lean — `--build-arg INCLUDE_OPTIONAL=true` re-includes `@xenova/transformers` + `natural` + `openai` when the consumer needs the local Transformers embedder OR the OpenAI providers (EC-39 absorbed honestly: optional deps are GATED, not always shipped); (3) `runtime` `gcr.io/distroless/nodejs22-debian12:nonroot` produces a 199.7MB image (< 200MB target per acceptance criterion) running as nonroot UID 65532 with no shell present (EC-47 distroless integrity verified — `/bin/sh: stat: no such file`). ENTRYPOINT invokes the Node binary directly per ADR D13: `["/nodejs/bin/node", "/app/dist/agent-tools/cli.js", "server"]`; no `docker-entrypoint.sh` — migrate-on-startup discipline lives inside `themory server` (T2.3); SIGTERM lands on Node-as-PID-1 natively (EC-45/46 obsoleted by removing the shell layer). Operator override for `migrate` / `health` standalone via `docker run --entrypoint /nodejs/bin/node ghcr.io/usetheo/themory:0.1.0-alpha /app/dist/agent-tools/cli.js {migrate|health}` documented in README. `.dockerignore` excludes audit trails (`.claude/`), examples, docs, benchmarks, tests, env files, drizzle-kit config so the build context stays small and secrets never leak into layers. Production compose at `docker-compose.prod.yaml` (T3.2) boots BOTH `themory` (image `ghcr.io/usetheo/themory:${VERSION:-0.1.0-alpha}` with `depends_on: pgvector { condition: service_healthy }`; healthcheck via Node http client probing `GET /v1/health` since distroless has no curl) AND `pgvector` (`ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1` convergent across + per blueprint Q7; healthcheck via `pg_isready`); EC-41 documents `docker compose up -d --wait --wait-timeout 60` operator usage; EC-42 absorbs fail-safe defaults for `VERSION` / `POSTGRES_*` / `THEMORY_PORT` so `docker compose config` exits 0 on cold clone without exporting anything; reverse proxy NOT bundled ( bundles Nginx; we don't per CLAUDE.md Roadmap Item 17 stance "consumer decides"). GitHub Actions workflow at `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml` (T3.3) triggers on tag-push `v*` (release path) / push to `main` (preview `:edge` + `:sha-<short>`) / `workflow_dispatch`; build/cache via `type=gha`; Trivy CRITICAL+HIGH gate with `exit-code: 1` aligned with `deps-audit-golden-rule.md` severity rubric; permissions declared at workflow + job level (`contents: read`, `packages: write`) per EC-44; `.trivyignore` at repo root (empty at v0.1.0-alpha) carries sunset-dated CVE allowlist entries when needed (mirror of `deps-audit-allowlist.txt` format). 5 tasks across Phase 3: T3.1 Dockerfile (`48af406`), T3.2 docker-compose.prod.yaml (`dd777a0`), T3.3 GHCR workflow + Trivy gate + .trivyignore (`7c65b0c`), T3.4 ENTRYPOINT verification + README override docs (this commit), T3.5 THE GOAL TEST docker-stack integration test follows. 13 new integration tests across Phase 3 pin the static-shape invariants (compose validates without VERSION env; workflow declares permissions; ENTRYPOINT direct Node + no docker-entrypoint.sh + CMD empty + README override pattern present + distroless base image). `/dogfood` gate remains EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT — Phase 3 does NOT unblock production-ready claims. (#TBD)

- **v0.1.0-alpha P0 Phase 2 — CLI consolidado `themory {mcp,server,migrate,health}` — Roadmap Item 18** — replaces the standalone `themory-mcp` bin with a multi-subcommand `themory` entry built on `commander ^12.1.0`. 4 subcommands: `themory mcp` (start MCP server; preserves v0.3 behavior with `--transport stdio|streamable-http`, `--port`, `--host` flags overriding the legacy `THEOMEM_MCP_*` env vars); `themory server` (start REST API server; `--port`, `--host`; runs Drizzle migrations on startup per ADR D8 unless `THEMEMORY_SKIP_MIGRATIONS=true` opts out; binds port EAGERLY while the Memory factory warms async so `GET /v1/health` honestly returns 503 `warming_up` per EC-33; SIGTERM/SIGINT idempotent graceful shutdown via shared handler closes HTTP server → drains pool → exit 0; EADDRINUSE surfaces clear stderr `Port N already in use` + exit 1); `themory migrate` (applies pending Drizzle migrations against `THEOMEM_PG_URI` with idempotent journal-locked re-run per EC-36); `themory health` (readiness probe — mandatory DB `SELECT 1` within 2s timeout per EC-17 + Memory factory composition check; optional `--deep` flag invokes `embedder.embed("ping")` + `llm.generateChat(...)` for round-trip smoke validation). Backward-compat alias `themory-mcp` retained for one release via `package.json#bin` dual entry pointing to the same compiled CLI; alias detection regex `/themory-mcp(?:\.(?:js|mjs|cjs|ts))?$/` on `process.argv[1]` covers npm exec wrapper / npx temp / Windows paths / direct invocations; emits one-time DEPRECATED stderr warning then mutates `process.argv` via idempotent splice (`process.argv[2] === 'mcp'` skips re-injection) per EC-27/EC-29. cli.ts ends at 158 LoC (under 200 LoC budget per plan) thanks to per-subcommand composition-root extraction (`src/agent-tools/run-mcp-server.ts` T2.1, `run-server.ts` T2.3, `run-migrate.ts` T2.4, `run-health.ts` T2.5 — SRP discipline; cli.ts owns routing only). EC-7 hard-fail on absent `THEOMEM_PG_URI` (exit 2 + stderr example URI) unified across all 4 wired actions. `CreateServerOptions` widened with `readinessProbe?:  => boolean` consulted by `healthHandler` per EC-33 (plan-scope expansion: extends `src/server/handlers/health.ts` + `src/server/app.ts`; documented in SEPA iter 11 log). 5 tasks across Phase 2: T2.1 commander scaffold + alias detection (`20c8300`), T2.2 mcp wired (`f423fe6`), T2.3 server (`c4fd50b`), T2.4 migrate (`f6a0bbb`), T2.5 health (this commit). v0.3 e2e regression `tests/e2e/agent-tools/round-trip.test.ts` PASS via updated `spawn('node', [CLI_PATH, 'mcp'])` invocation (legacy `node CLI_PATH` shape is covered by EC-27 alias path tests). 941 contract + 8 integration tests PASS (+30 across Phase 2). Phase 3 (Docker stack distroless + GHCR + GitHub Actions — Roadmap Item 17) follows. `/dogfood` gate remains EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT — this commit does NOT unblock production-ready claims. (#TBD)

- **v0.1.0-alpha P0 Phase 1 — REST API server (`@usetheo/memory/server`) — Roadmap Item 16** — exposes the 4 verbs (`POST /v1/{remember,recall,forget,reflect}`) + `GET /v1/health` (readiness) + `GET /v1/health/db` (DB liveness 2s timeout) + `GET /v1/scopes/:type/:id/memories` (paginated listing with two-layer cursor) over HTTP via Hono ^4.6.0. Bearer-token auth via `THEMEMORY_API_KEY` env (RFC 6750; case-insensitive scheme per EC-2; `timingSafeEqual` with length-pre-check per EC-4). OpenAPI 3.1 spec auto-derived from zod schemas at `GET /v1/openapi.json` auth-exempt per EC-19. OpenTelemetry spans per handler (`rest.v1.remember|recall|forget|reflect|health|scopes.list`) with `http.status_code`/`http.method`/`http.route` attributes; tracer injected via `createServer({ memory, tracer })` per ADR D11 DI composition; default falls through to global no-op tracer (zero cost when consumer has no OTel provider). Domain-error → HTTP envelope mapping at `src/server/errors.ts::mapDomainErrorToResponse` (pure function; 9 typed errors mapped to 404/413/422/501/503 with `{code,message,field?}` envelope; default 500 sanitized so raw `err.message` never echoes to body — EC-23 pg error absorption + EC-24 `bearer\s+\S+` redaction before `console.error`). Single scope tier per request enforced via zod `superRefine` (EC-9 — not plain union) so a body with both `userId` and `sessionId` returns 422 INVALID_SCOPE. 12th DIP `AuthVerifier` at `src/core/dip/auth-verifier.ts` for future OAuth/JWT swap. Subpath export `./server` added to `package.json#exports` (ESM-only per EC-25). EC-26 bundle isolation: default `@usetheo/memory` barrel does NOT transitively reach `src/server/**` (regression guard at `tests/integration/subpath-isolation.test.ts` via recursive static-import walk — no esbuild needed). EC-52 `npm pack --dry-run` ships all `src/server/**` source files (verified via parsed JSON output). 4 NEW deps pinned with sunset 2026-09-01 per ADR D14 (`hono ^4.6.0`, `@hono/node-server ^1.13.0`, `@hono/zod-openapi ^0.18.0`, `@apidevtools/swagger-parser ^10.1.1`). 7 tasks across Phase 1: T0.1 deps scaffold (`c101b3f`), T1.1 bearer-auth middleware + AuthVerifier DIP (`6bbc845`), T1.2 4-verb handlers + ADR D12 single-tier enforcement (`09f97ff`), T1.3 health + scopes pagination (`befd9aa`), T1.4 OpenAPI 3.1 auto-gen via hybrid `OpenAPIHono` registration (`7d2e365`), T1.5 OTel span middleware + DI + EC-22 fail-quiet (`9f664ad`), T1.6 domain-error envelope + EC-23/24 (`05c268a`), T1.7 subpath export + isolation + EC-25/26/52 (this commit). 911 contract + integration tests PASS (+62 new across Phase 1). README documents the new surface at `## REST API server`. `/dogfood` gate remains EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT — this commit does NOT unblock production-ready claims; Roadmap Item 11 v1.0 gate still requires anchor scenario "Claude Code auto-memory backed by TheMemory" running ≥ 30 days with evidence logs. Phase 2 (CLI consolidado `themory {mcp,server,migrate,health}` — Roadmap Item 18) + Phase 3 (Docker stack distroless + GHCR — Roadmap Item 17) follow. (#TBD)

- **v1.1 — Multi-peer attribution + Bi-temporal local-runtime lift (Roadmap row 12 + ADR-0011; plan `v1-1-multi-peer-and-bi-temporal-plan.md` v1.2 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 89.6/100)** — ships `attributed_to` as a first-class promoted column on `memories` (migration 0008 + Drizzle schema + `MemoryItem.attributedTo` DTO + `RememberOptions.attributedTo` verbatim-path widening per EC-2 + `RecallOptions.attributedTo` filter widening) AND lifts `BiTemporalNotSupportedLocally` on the local runtime so `recall({ asOf })` returns memories valid at a historical instant via the -precedent null-safe predicate `(t_valid IS NULL OR t_valid <= asOf) AND (t_invalid IS NULL OR t_invalid > asOf)`. **THE GOAL TEST** `tests/integration/v1-1-multi-peer-bitemporal.test.ts` returns 7/7 PASS against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`. **ADR-0011 (supplementary to ADR-0006)** authorizes the runtime-clause supersession (schema clause reinforced by Apache-2.0 prior art per blueprint Q6); project-owner sign-off recorded in commit `ee6056d` via AskUserQuestion at /implement Gate 2. CLAUDE.md Roadmap row 7 narrowed: cloud-premium scope drops bi-temporal (now v1.1 local OSS) and focuses on multi-region replication + audit-mgmt. **N-valent attribution semantic** per blueprint Q5 — LLM extraction prompt relaxed from TRINARY (`"user|system|agent"`) to arbitrary peer IDs, attacking 's AGPL-3.0 vertical moat under TheMemory MIT without code reuse (pattern only). 13 EC absorptions across 8 tasks: EC-1 honest no-backfill clarification (pre-v1.1 pipeline at `src/core/extraction/pipeline.ts:285 buildPayload` discarded `extracted[i].attributed_to`; historical rows carry NULL permanently; documented in ADR D2 Consequences); EC-2 RememberOptions.attributedTo widening for verbatim `infer:false` callers; EC-3 boundary validation throws `InvalidScope('asOf must be a valid Date')` when `Number.isNaN(asOf.getTime)` per architecture.md § 8; EC-4 stale-references audit (39 actionable hits across src/ cleared in T3.1; re-grep returns zero non-deliberate matches); EC-5 entity-boost filter consistency (single composeFilters clause covers the only entity-boost path post-ADR-0007 disable); EC-6 migration 0008 idempotency (ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS + CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS — 2x runMigrations no-op verified in integration test); EC-7 empty-string vs NULL Postgres semantics (`'' = ''` matches; `NULL = ''` excluded); EC-8 stub LLM fixture stability post-prompt-relax; EC-9 `composeTemporalWhere` helper available at the SQL layer for all SELECT call sites; **however the skill-scope public API (`memory.skills(repoId).recall(...)`) does NOT accept `asOf` in v1.1** — `SkillRecallOptions` declares only `{ topK, threshold, includeSuperseded }`; skill-scope `asOf` deferred to a follow-up cycle per ADR D4 amendment 2026-06-03 (see plan v1.3); EC-10 concurrent dedup-hash collision keeps first writer's attribution per existing `ON CONFLICT (hash) DO NOTHING`; EC-11 GDPR caveat documented in `RecallOptions.asOf` JSDoc — soft-deleted memories recoverable via historical asOf; GDPR-strict erasure REQUIRES `forget(id, { hard: true })` per v0.4b shipped 2026-05-31; EC-12 skill rows inherit `attributed_to=NULL` (YAGNI per Rule 11); EC-13 TheoKit binding + examples UI updates deferred to v1.2. **Public API additive widenings:** `MemoryItem.attributedTo?: string`, `RememberOptions.attributedTo?: string`, `RecallOptions.attributedTo?: string`, `Filters.attributed_to?: string`, `Filters.asOf?: Date`. ADR-0008 method-count cap preserved (zero new methods on any DIP; only optional param widenings on existing options bags). `BiTemporalNotSupportedLocally` class still EXPORTED per ADR-0011 Consequences (cloud retains the option to throw; class marked `@deprecated for local`). **Migration discipline:** EC-1 documents the honest contract — pre-v1.1 rows have `attributed_to=NULL` permanently because the prior pipeline discarded the LLM-extracted value. `/deps-audit` PASS_WITH_CAVEATS (cap 89 via the pre-existing drizzle-kit allowlisted MODERATE GHSA-67mh-4wv8-2f99 sunset 2026-08-23; ZERO new deps introduced — Q1+Q2 EC-8 finding from the multi-peer-and-bi-temporal-gaps blueprint confirmed bi-temporal in OSS requires no specialized vendor lib). **Tests:** 5 new contract tests T1.1 + 4 T1.2 + 6 T2.1 + 4 T2.2 + 5 T3.1+T3.2 = +24 contract tests on top of the v0.8 baseline. 845/845 full contract suite PASS. **Stale-references audit cleared:** `reviews/v1-1-stale-refs-2026-06-02.md` enumerated 39 actionable Category-A hits; T3.1 cleared all bi-temporal stale-ref text (`src/core/options.ts` JSDoc + `src/local/schema.ts` header + `src/local/index.ts` recall JSDoc + `src/agent-tools/tools/recall-schema.ts` v0.5+ comment + `src/local/pgvector-store.ts` consolidation guard wording + `src/local/__tests__/factory.test.ts` test descriptor); re-grep against `"throws BiTemporalNotSupportedLocally\|until v0.5\|v0.5+ cloud\|bi-temporal asOf surface v0.5"` returns ZERO non-deliberate matches. **Commits:** T0.1 (`ee6056d` — ADR-0011 + ADR-0006 amendment + CLAUDE.md Roadmap row 7 + EC-4 stale-refs audit) + T1.1 (`ef4a434` — migration 0008 + schema + DTO + RememberOptions widening + EC-1/EC-2/EC-6 absorbed) + T1.2 (`7f4eb05` — LLM prompt N-valent relax + EC-8) + T2.1 (`24d4f05` — pgvector-store hoist + projection + pipeline wiring + EC-2 verbatim + EC-10 dedup + EC-1 closed for new writes) + T2.2 (`73cd889` — RecallOptions.attributedTo filter + composeFilters WHERE + EC-5 + EC-7) + T3.1+T3.2 (`8876fb3` — lift throw + asOf SQL composer + EC-3 boundary + EC-4 audit closure + EC-9 skill rows + EC-11 GDPR caveat + factory.test.ts assertion rewrite) + T4.1 (this commit — integration test + CHANGELOG). **`/dogfood` gate remains independent and EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT — this commit does NOT unblock production-ready claims.** Plan archive deferred until `/review` returns READY_TO_MERGE per cycle-plan archive discipline.

THE FINAL GOAL TEST `tests/e2e/benchmarks/matrix-publication.test.ts` validates 12-cell matrix + no literal "TBD" + every cell is Smoke or Deferred+DriftRef.
