- **v0.7 fix-up cycle (plan v1.2 — closes the 3 CRITICAL findings from `/review v0-7-distillation-pipeline`)** — `/review v0-7-distillation-pipeline` 2026-06-02 spawned 7 reviewers in parallel; consolidated report (`reviews/v0-7-distillation-pipeline-review-2026-06-02.md`) returned NEEDS_FIXES with 3 CRITICAL + 11 MAJOR + 12 MINOR findings. **Plan bumped to v1.2** with explicit dismissal of T2.1 cosine-clustering precedent in favor of per-policy mass-tombstone guard (ADR D2 amendment). **Three CRITICAL fixes:** (F-C3) `consolidation_history` now declared as `pgTable` export in `src/local/schema.ts` (was only in migration + meta snapshot — `drizzle-kit push` workflow would have computed a destructive `DROP TABLE` diff and silently lost all GDPR audit rows). Closure: commit `9d16db4` adds the declaration + `tests/integration/schema-snapshot-parity.test.ts` sentinel asserting every snapshot table is mirrored in schema.ts (bijection both directions; 2/2 PASS). (F-C2 + F-M7) `recallConsolidationCandidates` now filters `kind = 'extracted' AND (t_valid IS NULL OR t_valid <= NOW)` — previously v0.6 skill rows + already-consolidated `kind='derived'` rows entered the cluster, allowing reflect({ conflictResolution }) on an agent_id scope to mass-tombstone the skill library. Closure: commit `ffa92d1` adds the SQL guard + `tests/integration/distillation/skill-isolation.test.ts` (2/2 PASS — skill rows survive LWW reflect, derived rows not re-consolidated). (F-C1) `LastWriteWinsConflictPolicy` gains `LastWriteWinsConflictPolicyOptions.maxRejectionRatio` constructor option (default `0.95`) that ABSTAINS when LWW would reject more than 95% of candidates. Empirical line: 20 candidates with 19 rejected (ratio 0.95) consolidates; 21+ candidates with all-but-one rejected (ratio ~0.952+) abstains. Blocks egregious 50-row → 49 deleted mass-tombstone while preserving normal 2-20 semantic-duplicate consolidation. Opt-out via `{ maxRejectionRatio: 1.0 }` for callers who have already scope-isolated the candidate set. Closure: commit `17425af` adds the guard + 5 new contract tests (10/10 PASS). Contract suite: 685 passed (was 680, +5 F-C1 guard). **Selective MAJOR/MINOR closures:** F-M5 LLM-MEASURED test header gains explicit "what this proves vs what it does NOT" caveat (10 categorically-distinct hand-authored fixtures say nothing about ambiguous-contradiction quality; v0.7.2 follow-up would author 50+ ambiguous fixtures); F-Mi2 `/api/memory/distill` route now calls `getMemory` BEFORE the `OPENAI_API_KEY` check so the OpenRouter env-shift fires (no spurious 422 in local-openrouter mode). **Deferred with rationale:** F-M1/M2 (file-size budget), F-M3/M4 (OTel integration depth), F-M6 (wiring locus drift), F-M8 (baseline T3.3 LLM-quality), F-M9 (outer reflect atomicity), F-M10/M11 (FK naming + ON UPDATE NO ACTION — cosmetic; rename requires new migration for deployed DBs).
- **v0.7.1 LLM-MEASURED GOAL TEST — closes ADR-EC-B (`tests/integration/distillation/the-goal-test-llm-measured.test.ts`)** — runs `LlmJudgedMergeConflictPolicy` against `openai/gpt-4o-mini` via OpenRouter on the 10 hand-authored `contradiction-pair` fixtures. Result: **precision=1.000, recall=1.000, conflict-resolution accuracy=1.000 (10/10 attempted, ~21s wall-clock, ~$0.01-0.05 LLM cost)** — all three metrics exceed the v0.7 acceptance thresholds (≥ 0.75 / 0.70 / 0.80). Closes the LLM-measured deferral that v0-7-distillation-pipeline plan v1.1 ADR-EC-B authorized. Reproducibility: `OPENROUTER_(API_)KEY=sk-or-v1-... THEOMEM_PG_URI=postgresql://... npx vitest run tests/integration/distillation/the-goal-test-llm-measured.test.ts --config vitest.integration.config.ts`. Schema-dim adaptive: probes `pg_attribute.atttypmod` at boot so the same test passes against either a 384-dim demo DB or the default 1536. Matrix entry persisted in `docs/EVALUATION.md` § v0.7.1 LLM-MEASURED. Roadmap Item 9 (CLAUDE.md § Phase III) status flipped from `Planned` to `Shipped 2026-06-02` with the v0.7.1 numbers + caveat that procedural-fixture quality remains untested at the LLM-measured surface (deferred to v0.7.2 if needed) and `/review` formal pass is still pending.
- **adapters-openrouter-local-embedder fix-up cycle (v1.2 amendments closing /review findings)** — `/review adapters-openrouter-local-embedder` 2026-06-02 spawned 7 reviewers in parallel; consolidated report (`reviews/adapters-openrouter-local-embedder-review-2026-06-02.md`) returned NEEDS_FIXES with 1 CRITICAL + 15 MAJOR + 12 MINOR findings. **Plan bumped to v1.2** with explicit ADR D3 amendment (DELETE+ALTER replaces USING NULL recipe; pgvector NOT NULL constraint collision empirically verified) + EC-B amendment (cascade collateral disclosure). **Honesty alignment (F-C1 CRITICAL + F-M2 MAJOR closed):** the destructive dim change wipes BROADER than originally disclosed — the `DELETE FROM memories` triggers `history.memory_id ON DELETE CASCADE` (entire history audit trail), `consolidation_history.kept_memory_id ON DELETE SET NULL` (v0.7 distillation provenance pointers), and `memories.superseded_by` self-FK ON DELETE SET NULL (v0.6 supersession chains). README destructive-dim caveat enumerated the full cascade graph; SDK JSDoc + console.error warning text updated to match. **Test gaps closed:** EC-3 atomic rollback regression test added (F-M1); DELETE-cascade-behavior test added (F-M6); push-flow path TDD test added (F-M7 — closes T4.1 follow-up empirical-discovery-not-RED-tested gap); /api/memory/distill consolidation_history SELECT assertion added (F-M8); /api/chat 384-dim memory insert end-to-end test added (F-M9); EC-5 empty-string + EC-7 warmup network failure + EC-10 missing OPENAI_BASE_URL warn tests added (F-M12). **Code corrections:** asymmetric backward-compat guard removed at `src/local/index.ts` (F-M4 — let inner atttypmod probe handle idempotency on 384→1536 revert); LocalTransformerEmbedder rejected-promise singleton reset (F-M5 — first-call failure no longer poisons subsequent calls until process restart); stale `atttypmod = N+4` block comment in dim-change.test.ts removed (F-M10); orphan-schema warning text updated from misleading "skipping runMigrations" to honest "skipping migrate; applying explicit dim change only" (F-M13); PgvectorStore.insert/insertBatch/insertDerivedMemory/update gained the same dim guard symmetry that .search has (F-M15 + F-Mi6 — typed EmbeddingMismatch instead of bare Error); new dedicated `InvalidEmbeddingDim` error class replaces InvalidScope reuse (F-Mi4); CREATE INDEX statements use quoted identifiers (F-Mi9); test naming hygiene fixes (F-Mi11 + F-Mi12). **F-M3 (Drizzle schema.ts hardcoded 1536) mitigated** via document-only defense-in-depth (JSDoc on `src/local/schema.ts` + runtime `console.error` in `applyDimChange`) instructing the operator NEVER to run `drizzle-kit push` against a DB while a non-1536 embeddingDim is active. Full fix (parametric schema.ts at build time OR runtime schema validation) deferred to a follow-up `/to-plan parametric-drizzle-schema` cycle since proper fix touches SDK build pipeline. **F-M11 BREAKING (theoretical)** documented separately below.

- **BREAKING (theoretical) — PgvectorStore constructor 2nd arg widened from positional `tracer?: Tracer` to options object `{ embeddingDim?, tracer? }`** (T0.2 commit `804f2fa`; F-M11 surfaced in /review 2026-06-02). Empirical sweep at iter-2 SEPA pre-RED confirmed ZERO callers across SDK + tests + examples + node_modules use the positional-tracer form, but any external consumer of `@usetheo/memory` passing `new PgvectorStore(pool, tracer)` would have the tracer SILENTLY IGNORED. Migration for external consumers: change `new PgvectorStore(pool, myTracer)` to `new PgvectorStore(pool, { tracer: myTracer })`. Backward-compat preserved for the dominant `new PgvectorStore(pool)` form (the only form used in-tree).

- **adapters-openrouter-local-embedder cycle — parametric `embeddingDim` + `LocalTransformerEmbedder` + `local-openrouter` demo backend** (`adapters-openrouter-local-embedder-plan.md` v1.1 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 83.6). Unblocks the `examples/chat-with-memory/` demo for OpenRouter-only API keys: chat completions route through OpenRouter via OpenAI Node SDK's native `OPENAI_BASE_URL` support (ZERO SDK code change per ADR D5); embeddings run locally via `@xenova/transformers` ^2.17.0 since OpenRouter does NOT proxy `/v1/embeddings`. **SDK additive surface (Phase 0 — backward-compatible):** `LocalMemoryConfig.embeddingDim?: number` (default `MAX_EMBEDDING_DIM=1536`) with `Number.isInteger` pre-flight guard catching NaN/float/Infinity per EC-1 absorption — error includes `String(received)` for operator diagnosability. `PgvectorStore` constructor 2nd arg widened from positional `tracer?: Tracer` to options object `{ embeddingDim?, tracer? }` (sweep at SEPA iter-2 confirmed zero positional-tracer callers). `runMigrations(pool, { embeddingDim? })` accepts the new option; when dim differs from 1536, runs atomic DROP+ALTER+RECREATE sequence (`DROP INDEX entities_embedding_hnsw_idx + memories_embedding_hnsw_idx` → 2 `ALTER COLUMN TYPE vector(N) USING NULL` → 2 `CREATE INDEX ... USING hnsw vector_cosine_ops`) inside a single BEGIN/COMMIT with ROLLBACK on catch (EC-3 atomic + EC-4 HNSW chicken-and-egg absorption). Idempotency short-circuit via `pg_attribute.atttypmod` probe (empirically corrected — pgvector v0.5.1 stores N directly, NOT N+4 as SEPA training-data claimed). **New SDK export (Phase 1):** `LocalTransformerEmbedder` at `@usetheo/memory/embedders/local` (subpath-only per ADR D7 mirroring stub + openai precedents). Lazy Promise singleton (race-safe per JS single-thread invariant); pipeline call passes `{ pooling: 'mean', normalize: true }` so output is L2-normalized (cosine distance well-defined; matches `DeterministicStubEmbedder` invariant). Default model `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2` (384-dim) cached at `~/.cache/transformers-js/` on first `embed` call (~22 MB one-time download). `@xenova/transformers` declared in `optionalDependencies` per ADR D1 + Cycle 2 EC-6 precedent. **Demo singleton (Phase 2):** `examples/chat-with-memory/lib/memory.ts` extended with new `THEOMEM_DEMO_BACKEND={stub|openai|local-openrouter}` env var (canonical selector; legacy `THEOMEM_USE_STUB_ADAPTERS=1` sunset 2026-09-01). New exported helper `resolveBackendMode` with EC-11 unknown-value loud-fail (e.g., underscore typo `local_openrouter` throws explicit Error listing 3 valid values — silent fallthrough would mislead operator). `MemorySingleton` interface widened: `adapterSource: 'stub' | 'openai' | 'local-openrouter'` + new `embedderDim: number` field. `getConflictResolutionPolicy('llm-judged-merge')` guard at line 269 naturally widens correctly (existing `=== 'stub'` check passes local-openrouter through since underlying LLM is OpenAILLM routed via OpenRouter supporting JSON-judged-merge). `/api/info` route extended to return `adapterSource` + `embedderDim`; sidebar header adds a second blue pill showing `adapter (dim)` (e.g., `local-openrouter (384)`). **EC-13 absorption:** new unit tests at `tests/unit/examples/chat-with-memory-memory.test.ts` + `chat-with-memory-info-route.test.ts` (matches `tests/unit/**/*.test.ts` vitest contract glob — NOT under `examples/.../__tests__/` which would be unreachable per vitest.contract.config.ts). `vitest.contract.config.ts` extended with `resolve.alias` for `server-only` (Next.js build-time guard shim) + `@/lib/memory` (matches `examples/chat-with-memory/tsconfig.json#paths`). **CRITICAL — deps-audit halt + allowlist:** post-install `npm audit --json` against `@xenova/transformers` ^2.17.0 surfaced FAIL_INSECURE (1 CRITICAL + 4 HIGH on `protobufjs <= 7.5.7` via `@xenova/transformers` → `onnxruntime-web@1.14.0` pinned → `onnx-proto` → `protobufjs`). Cycle-implement.md Stop Condition #5 triggered; ralph-loop halted at iter-4 with honest BLOCKED report. **User-approved allowlist** landed at commit `6693ea4` with aggressive sunset (CRITICAL GHSA-xq3m-2v4x-88gg → 2026-07-01; 4 HIGH advisories → 2026-08-01; 3 umbrella entries `@xenova/transformers` + `onnxruntime-web` + `onnx-proto` → 2026-07-01). Risk envelope documented in `.claude/rules/deps-audit-allowlist.txt`: protobufjs parser invoked ONLY at model warmup (one-time .onnx download from `huggingface.co/Xenova` CDN); runtime inference path does NOT route user input through it. Demo-only scope; dogfood-golden-rule blocks any production-ready claim while entries are active. Re-audit at sunset; expected upstream fix via Xenova v3 line. **Documentation:** `examples/chat-with-memory/README.md` adds `## Backend modes` section with 3-mode table + OpenRouter recipe + first-use cache caveat + destructive-dim caveat; `## Optional env` table extended with `THEOMEM_DEMO_BACKEND` + `OPENAI_BASE_URL` rows; `THEOMEM_USE_STUB_ADAPTERS` marked _Legacy — sunset 2026-09-01_. **`.env.example` update DEFERRED** (file is hook-protected; operator updates manually following README guidance). **Tests:** 12 RED→GREEN tests for `resolveBackendMode` precedence + EC-11 unknown-value variants; 4 RED→GREEN tests for `/api/info` extended shape; 5 RED→GREEN integration tests for `runMigrations` parametric path against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`; 9 RED→GREEN tests for `PgvectorStore({ embeddingDim? })` constructor + search guard; 9 RED→GREEN tests for `LocalMemoryConfig.embeddingDim` + EC-1 guard variants; 7 RED→GREEN tests for `LocalTransformerEmbedder` DIP contract (dim 384, L2 unit norm, determinism, semantic discrimination, empty batch shortcut, batch order, embed/embedBatch agreement); 2 subpath-resolution tests at `tests/integration/public-paths.test.ts`. 658/660 full contract suite + 12/12 all migration integration tests PASS. **Phase 0 SDK foundations: T0.1 (`e36faea`) + T0.2 (`804f2fa`) + T0.3 (`e68a38a`). Recovery: allowlist (`6693ea4`). Phase 1: T1.1 (`c488734`) + T1.2 (`8acdf8b`). Phase 2: T2.1 (`c5425ec`) + T2.2 (`94e43a5`). Phase 3: T3.1 (this commit). T4.1 integration test = next commit.** Cycle has 1 user-decision recovery point on record (allowlist), 1 empirical SEPA correction (atttypmod encoding), 1 cycle-implement.md anti-pattern logged-not-violated (C1 of SEPA iter-2: plan T0.2 § Files to edit #3 was wrong; T2.1 not createLocalMemory plumbs the dim — documented for `/review` architecture-reviewer).

- **examples/chat-with-memory v0.7 distillation tab + HTTP surface** (consumer-side wiring of the just-shipped v0.7 SDK into the Next.js demo; `examples-chat-with-memory-v0-7-distillation-plan.md` v1.1 SHIPPABLE 94.0). New `DistillationTab` (6th sidebar tab, after `Eviction`) lets the operator pick one of the 3 conflict-resolution policies (`noop` / `last-write-wins` / `llm-judged-merge`) via 3-radio selector + click `Distill now` + see the `consolidation_history` audit rows populate in a paginated panel. **New routes:** `POST /api/memory/distill` (127 LoC; body `{userId, policy}` → `{derivedMemoriesCreated, reason, invocationId}`) + `GET /api/memory/consolidation-history` (86 LoC; default `limit=50`, max `200`; ORDER BY `created_at DESC`; INNER JOIN scope filter). **New factory:** `getConflictResolutionPolicy(name: PolicyName)` in `lib/memory.ts` (closes over the singleton's `llm` instance for LJM construction per ADR D5; lazy — no eager OpenAI client init when only Noop/LWW are exercised). Public exports: `PolicyName`, `StubLlmNotUsableForLjmError`. **MemorySingleton extended** with `llm` + `adapterSource` fields (per SEPA pre-RED CRITICAL absorption — the original singleton shape didn't carry the LLM, defeating ADR D5's "closes over the singleton's llm field" claim). **5 edge cases absorbed:** EC-1 — when `THEOMEM_USE_STUB_ADAPTERS=1` + LJM requested, factory throws typed `StubLlmNotUsableForLjmError`; route maps to `422 { code: 'STUB_LLM_NOT_USABLE_FOR_LJM' }` (Rule 3 + ADR D3: NO silent fallback would lie about which policy ran). EC-2 — integration test bootstrap uses `describe.skipIf(!reachable || PG_URI === '')` against a top-level `await isReachable('localhost:3000/api/health')` probe (cleanly skips in CI without manual server; mirrors EC-11 pattern from v0.7 SDK eval doc). EC-3 — POST validates `policy` against the literal union BEFORE invoking the factory → `400 { error: 'invalid policy; expected one of ...' }` (not `500`; defense-in-depth path stays in the factory). EC-4 — concurrent POSTs run as independent invocations per v0.7 SDK T2.1 contract; first commits the soft-delete + audit row; second sees the singleton cluster and early-exits. EC-5 — `DistillationTab` `useEffect` cleanup uses `AbortController.abort` on `userId` dep change so stale fetch results cannot overwrite current state. EC-6 — `disabled={loading}` on the `Distill now` button covers both the inflight POST and the post-POST audit-list refresh (rapid double-clicks bounce off). EC-7 — every click writes new audit rows by design (no client-side dedup; pagination prevents render blowup). EC-8 — rows with `kept_memory_id IS NULL` (post-`forget({hard:true})`) excluded from the tab view via INNER JOIN; raw SQL example documented for operators. EC-B — `422 { code: 'OPENAI_API_KEY_MISSING' }` when LJM + no env key. EC-D — `crypto.randomUUID` invocationId logged at `[distill] start` + `[distill] end` so concurrent + race scenarios stay traceable in demo logs. **vitest.integration.config.ts** extended with `resolve.alias` for `server-only` → `tests/integration/examples/server-only-shim.ts` (Next.js build-time guard makes the import throw outside server runtime; alias lets integration tests import `lib/memory.ts` without tripping the guard). **Test suite:** `tests/integration/examples/chat-with-memory-policy-factory.test.ts` (7 unit-shape tests for the factory + extended MemorySingleton) + `tests/integration/examples/chat-with-memory-distillation.test.ts` (9 HTTP-layer tests against the live dev server + real pgvector). **HTTP fail-gracefully:** the route catches any thrown error from `reflect` post-consolidation (e.g., `DeterministicStubLLM` returns non-JSON `stub-response:<hex>` from `generateChat`) and records `reason='reflect-llm-failed-after-consolidation'`; consolidation work is already persisted per v0.7 SDK T2.1 order. **README** § "Distillation tab — v0.7 ConflictResolutionPolicy demo" documents the operator walk-through + EC-1 + EC-7 + EC-8 + UI-test BLOCKED-pending-Chrome-MCP disclosure. **`F-T4.1-1` Chrome MCP lock** carries over: HTTP-layer integration test is the load-bearing regression guard until Chrome MCP unblocks. **Zero new deps** verified via `/deps-audit` PASS_WITH_CAVEATS (6 pre-existing MODERATE allowlisted carry-forward — 4 root drizzle-kit chain sunset 2026-08-23 + 2 demo next/postcss chain sunset 2026-08-29). **Plan:** `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/examples-chat-with-memory-v0-7-distillation-plan.md` v1.1 SHIPPABLE 94.0/100. **Plan deviation documented:** T1.1 response shape omits the planned `consolidations: ConflictResolutionResult[]` field — `ReflectionResult` does not carry consolidations and adding `invocation_id` to `consolidation_history` would be a schema change; UI fetches the audit list via T1.2 GET after the POST instead. `invocationId` is emitted in the POST response + logs for correlation. **Commits:** T0.1 (`039aacb`) + T1.1+T1.2 (`f87a8a8`) + T2.1+T2.2+T3.1+T4.1 (this commit).

- **v0.7 — Episodic→Semantic distillation pipeline (Roadmap Item 9, Open Problem #3)** — first OSS implementation of the `ConflictResolutionPolicy` DIP with 3 built-in strategies. **API:** `Memory.reflect({ conflictResolution: <Policy> })` accepts a new `ReflectOptions.conflictResolution` field (`ConflictResolutionPolicy` interface). After the existing v0.4a derivation step, the wire layer (T2.1) recalls candidates from `memories` (real `created_at` via `recallConsolidationCandidates(filters)`), invokes `policy.consolidate(candidates, { scope, observationDate })`, and persists results via `persistConsolidations(results)` (single `withTransaction` wrapping `t_invalid` UPDATE + `consolidation_history` INSERT; EC-8 atomic rollback verified by integration test). **3 policies (ADR D2):** `NoopConflictPolicy` (DEFAULT — returns `[]`, preserves ADD-only invariant), `LastWriteWinsConflictPolicy` (deterministic max-by-`createdAt` with EC-1 lex-DESC tie-break on `memoryId` for batch-persist scenarios; exports `compareCandidatesDesc` helper), `LlmJudgedMergeConflictPolicy` (one LLM call per cluster via the `LLM` DIP; EC-2 semantic-validates `kept_id ∈ clusterIds && rejected_ids ⊂ clusterIds && kept_id ∉ rejected_ids` via exported `parseConsolidationResponse(raw, clusterIds)` helper; EC-4 OTel counters (`distillation.consolidation.{kept,rejected,abstain,cluster_too_large}`) lazily resolved via module-scoped `Map<string, Counter>` cache per F-dom-6 / v0.4b T10.1 precedent at `src/local/eviction/pg-evictor.ts:56-75`; EC-6 cluster size > 20 → abstain + `cluster_too_large` counter; EC-7 empty-scope graceful no-op via existing v0.4a short-circuit). **Schema:** `consolidation_history` table via Drizzle migration `0007_distillation_columns.sql` with `kept_memory_id uuid REFERENCES memories(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` (EC-3 GDPR survival: `forget(memId, { hard: true })` per v0.4b `T7.1` preserves the audit row by nullifying the FK column). EC-9 honesty fix: `memories.derived_from` is ALREADY JSONB shipped v0.4a (`src/local/pgvector-store.ts:71`); v0.7 only ADDS the new table — no schema change to `memories`. Migration uses split-step CREATE TABLE + DO-block ALTER FK with `information_schema` catalog probe for true idempotency. **Repair script:** `scripts/db-repair-v0-7-partial-state.mjs` detects FK + table + indexes + journal state. **Rollback runbook:** `docs/RUNBOOK.md`. **Gold-set fixtures:** 200 episodes across 5 categories per ADR D4 hybrid path: 50 hand-authored (`source: "hand"`, 10/category) carry REAL semantic diversity; 150 procedural (`source: "procedural-seed"`, deterministic seeded LCG via `scripts/generate-distillation-fixtures.mjs`) for shape/scale validation. Loader at `tests/fixtures/distillation/loader.ts` Zod-validates each fixture. T3.3 measures precision/recall on the 50 hand-authored subset only. **Eval harness:** `npm run eval:distillation` mirrors v0.4b `T7.3` pattern; writes `docs/EVALUATION.md` matrix. **Metrics** at `docs/distillation-evaluation/metrics.ts`: `tokenize`, `bleuScore` (**EC-10 honest correction**: `natural.BleuScore` does NOT exist — plan EC-10 was fabricated; BLEU implemented INLINE using `natural.NGrams` + `natural.WordTokenizer` primitives, zero new deps), `entityOverlapJaccard`, `precision`, `recall`, `conflictResolutionAccuracy` (use Jaccard at threshold 0.5 because BLEU-4 is too strict on short distillation facts). **EC-11 pre-conditions documented verbatim** in `docs/distillation-evaluation/README.md`. **THE GOAL TEST** at `tests/integration/distillation/the-goal-test.test.ts`: precision ≥ 0.75 + recall ≥ 0.70 + accuracy ≥ 0.80 + EC-5 3-run stability. **ADR-DEFER-LLM-MEASURED-GOAL-TEST**: halt-loop lacks `OPENAI_API_KEY`; T3.3 ships with LWW deterministic oracle (trivially passes p=1.0/r=1.0/acc=1.0). Real LLM-measured signal deferred to v0.7.1. Plan EC-B explicitly allows. **Public exports:** `ConflictResolutionPolicy`, `ConflictResolutionResult`, `ConsolidationCandidate`, `ConsolidationContext`, `NoopConflictPolicy`, `LastWriteWinsConflictPolicy` + `compareCandidatesDesc`, `LlmJudgedMergeConflictPolicy` + `parseConsolidationResponse` + `LlmJudgedMergeConflictPolicyOptions` + `CONFLICT_MERGE_PROMPT` + `generateConflictMergePrompt`. All reachable through both `@usetheo/memory` and `@usetheo/memory/contract`. **5 new integration tests** + **6 new unit test files** (16 unit + 5 integration tests pass). **Zero new npm deps** (per ADR D6 + Inquebrável Rule 9). 8-task plan committed: T0.1 (`bab7a02`) + T1.1 (`77f3be4`) + T1.2 (`92334e0`) + T1.3 (`6b5ca31`) + T2.1 (`8b4e4d1`) + T3.1 (`8756fd2`) + T3.2 (`b4fc509`) + T3.3 (`f7262ed`). **Plan:** `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/v0-7-distillation-pipeline-plan.md` v1.1 SHIPPABLE 94.4. **Blueprint:** `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/v0-7-episodic-semantic-distillation-blueprint.md`. **Followups:** F-7-1 (`ConsolidationResult` → `ConflictResolutionResult` rename to avoid v0.6 collision) + F-7-2 (T1.1 test relocated to match precedent for tsc-enforced RED). **Honesty disclosure**: T3.3 expects SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS verdict on `/review`; real LLM quality validation requires v0.7.1.

### Changed

- **v1.1 — ADR-0006 runtime clause SUPERSEDED IN PART by ADR-0011** — local-runtime `recall({ asOf })` no longer throws `BiTemporalNotSupportedLocally`. The schema clause of ADR-0006 (`t_valid`/`t_invalid` TIMESTAMPTZ columns on `memories` since v0.1) is REINFORCED by Apache-2.0 prior art (blueprint Q6 snapshots `ad591ea1...`, `90ef10ce...`, `9a03507b...`). The runtime clause is amended via [`ADR-0011-bi-temporal-local-runtime-gate-lift.md`](./.claude/knowledge-base/adrs/ADR-0011-bi-temporal-local-runtime-gate-lift.md). `BiTemporalNotSupportedLocally` class still exported per ADR-0011 Consequences (forward-compat with cloud runtime; marked `@deprecated for local`). Pre-v1.1 consumers wrapping `recall({ asOf })` in `try/catch` will no longer hit the throw on the local runtime; the call resolves normally. EC-3 boundary validation now throws `InvalidScope('asOf must be a valid Date')` when `Number.isNaN(asOf.getTime)` per architecture.md § 8.

- **v1.1 — CLAUDE.md Roadmap row 7 narrowed** — v0.5 cloud-premium scope shifts from "bi-temporal as cloud-exclusive moat" to multi-region replication + audit-mgmt + managed embedding pricing. Bi-temporal queries move to v1.1 local OSS tier per ADR-0011. This re-positions the OSS vs paid-tier split — bi-temporal becomes commodity in our OSS line (matching /'s headline feature under MIT) while cloud differentiation focuses on operational concerns.

- **v1.1 — LLM extraction prompt vocabulary widened from TRINARY to N-VALENT** — `src/core/extraction/prompt.ts` `attributed_to` advisory wording changed from `"user|system|agent"` enum-like to `"<peer-id>"` accepting arbitrary string values. Pre-v1.1 callers handcrafting `attributed_to: 'user'` keep working (BIVALENT/TRINARY remain subsets of N-VALENT). Zod schema unchanged (already `z.string.optional` pre-v1.1). Stub LLM fixtures stability verified via EC-8 regression test.

### Fixed

- **v0-8-fixes-cycle-1 — closes /review v0-8-independent-benchmark-reproduction findings** (plan `v0-8-fixes-cycle-1-plan.md` v1.3 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 72.4). Absorbs 1 BLOCKER + 5 MAJOR + opportunistic MINORs from `reviews/v0-8-independent-benchmark-reproduction-review-2026-06-02.md`. 9 atomic task commits on `develop`. **F-tests-1 BLOCKER closed (T0.1 `f048bad`):** BLEU honest deferral via `Drift-T1.1-1` — `natural@8.1.x` empirically dropped BLEU entirely (`find node_modules/natural -name '*bleu*'` returned zero matches), so `calculateBleuScore` rewritten to throw `BleuMetricUnavailable` typed error carrying the marker text; `PerQueryRecord.bleu` + `RunAggregate.bleuMean` dropped; BLEU removed from runner pipeline + `docs/benchmarks/v0.8.md` matrix + methodology doc; `bleu.test.ts` rewritten from 6 silently-skipped tests to 3 unconditional throw-contract tests. **F-arch-1 MAJOR closed (T3.1 `faab853`):** `Tokenizer` interface promoted from `src/benchmarks/themory/adapter.ts` to `src/core/dip/tokenizer.ts` (11th DIP) — consumer count grew to 6 production + 5 test during T6.x vendor adapter rollout (Drift-T4.1-2 supersedes Drift-T4.1-1's "2 consumers" claim); 10 import paths updated; backward-compat re-export retained. 'unknown' coalesce) attributes per EC-8 (OTel spec rejects undefined values). **F-tests-2 MAJOR closed (T1.2 `fee163c`):** `buildSmokeJudgePanel(stubResponses?: readonly JudgeLabel[])` parameterized with EC-6 cycling-modulo-length contract; default `['CORRECT', 'WRONG']` so smoke runs exercise BOTH judgement paths; longmemeval-themory.test.ts asserts WRONG path is exercised. **F-tests-3 MAJOR closed (T1.3 `b868fc1`):** `download-fixtures.mjs` refactored to export `FIXTURES` + `getRemoteSize` + `getLocalSize` + `downloadToFile` + `downloadFixture` + `main` + `isMain` guard; NEW `download-fixtures.d.mts` for tsc + eslint; 7 RED tests covering EC-6 (Node 20+ fetch portability), EC-7 (`vi.unstubAllGlobals` cleanup), EC-8 (idempotent re-run via HEAD Content-Length match), EC-5 (config-parity sentinel verified post-vitest-glob expansion). **F-xval-1/2/3 + F-dom-mem-1 + F-tests-10 MAJOR/MINOR/INFO closed (T4.1 this commit):** source plan v1.2 stays FROZEN per EC-2 (`git diff main..HEAD -- v0-8-independent-benchmark-reproduction-plan.md` = 0); 5 drift markers (T1.1-1, T2.1-2, T4.1-2, T4.1-3, T8.x-2) persist in NEW sibling file `plans/v0-8-independent-benchmark-reproduction-drift-trail.md`; NEW `plans/v0-8-deferred-minors-trail.md` tracks 3 deferred MINORs (F-dom-mem-2 + F-dom-obs-3 + F-dom-obs-4 with sunset 2026-09-02) per ADR D8; 3 paper-name JSDoc violations cleaned (dmr-loader.ts, judge-prompt.ts, themory/adapter.ts); ADR D3 regression guard scope widened from `src/benchmarks/llm-judge/` files-only to `src/benchmarks/**/*.ts` recursive (skips `__tests__/`). **8 ADRs (D1-D8) with explicit `Alternatives considered` sub-section per /plan-confidence M2 contract.** Test impact: contract suite 800 PASS / 8 skipped → 823 PASS / 2 skipped (+23 net; -6 silently-skipped from F-tests-1 absorption; ~+29 new tests across 8 task commits). - **F-T4.1-2 eviction race — hotfix `b2909ff` lifted** (`f-t4-1-2-eviction-race-debug-plan.md` v1.1 SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 81.2). The hotfix in `examples/chat-with-memory/lib/memory.ts:resolveEviction` that disabled eviction wiring since 2026-05-31 is now reverted. SDK regression guard landed at `tests/integration/forgetting/f-t4-1-2-stub-reproduction.test.ts` — 3 race-attempt variants (T1.1 sequential single-scope 300 attempts + T1.2 overlapping multi-conversation 200 attempts + T1.3 SLOW-embedder simulating OpenAI latency 100 attempts = 600+ effective race attempts across 3 consecutive `npm run test:integration` runs) PASS 100% against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`. **HONEST FINDING (Inquebrável Rule 3):** the bug does NOT reproduce in pure-SDK with deterministic stubs OR with simulated OpenAI latency. Original observation in `b2909ff` was likely Next.js dev-mode HMR + globalThis singleton state mutation OR real-OpenAI-adapter timing characteristics that `setTimeout` doesn't faithfully simulate. Lifted with explicit advisory: if F-T4.1-2 resurfaces in demo runtime with real OpenAI, re-apply hotfix immediately + file follow-up cycle. T3.2 `/test-chat-with-memory` empirical re-validation BLOCKED via F-T4.1-1 (Chrome MCP lock); SDK integration test is the load-bearing regression guard. Cycle: `f-t4-1-2-eviction-race-debug` (8-task plan; T0.1 + T1.1 + T1.2 + T1.3 + T3.1 implemented; T2.1 = empirical no-op since T1.x PASSes; T3.2 = honest BLOCKED; T4.1 = validation gate + push).

### Added

- **OpenAI adapters extracted from demo to SDK** at `@usetheo/memory/embedders/openai` + `@usetheo/memory/llms/openai` (`adapters-openai-cycle-2-plan.md` v1.1 SHIPPABLE 90.8 — Roadmap Phase II Cycle 2; Adapters portfolio entry P0). MOVE-only lift (~92 LoC) from the demo's `examples/chat-with-memory/lib/openai-adapters.ts` into 2 first-class SDK files (`src/embedders/openai.ts` 70 LoC + `src/llms/openai.ts` 107 LoC); zero behavioral change per ADR D3. Same models (`text-embedding-3-small` 1536-dim model-implicit + `gpt-4o-mini`), same lazy-singleton client pattern (one OpenAI instance per module per process — duplicated across the 2 SDK files per EC-8 absorption to avoid scope-creep on a shared module), same `response_format` routing, same opaque `{error:'non_json_response', raw:content}` JSON-parse error shape preserved verbatim per EC-10. `_tools?: readonly unknown[]` 3rd param added to `OpenAILLM.generateResponse` to match `src/core/dip/llm.ts:LLM.generateResponse` (verified pre-flight via EC-4 — matches Cycle 1 stub precedent at `src/llms/stub.ts:106`). New dep: `openai: ^4.77.0` as `optionalDependencies` (per ADR D2 + Inquebrável Rule 9; `npm install --no-optional` skips the install for stub-only consumers; pin matches the demo's existing pin to prevent silent `npm install` resolution regression per `/deps-audit` advisory). 2 new subpath exports in `package.json#exports` (now 6 embedders+llms subpaths total). EC-3 root-no-reexport regression guard extended in `src/__tests__/no-root-reexport.test.ts` (asserts `root.OpenAIEmbedder` + `root.OpenAILLM` are undefined — locks the discipline before adapter #3 ships). EC-2 absorption: contract smoke tests at `src/{embedders,llms}/__tests__/openai.test.ts` assert DIP shape (class defined + `typeof method === 'function'`) WITHOUT calling `.embed` / `.embedBatch` / `.generateChat` / `.generateResponse` — would cost real tokens + flake CI without `OPENAI_API_KEY`; integration tests deferred to consumer dogfood (the chat-with-memory demo IS the real-API surface) per ADR D5. EC-1 pre-flight verified the demo had exactly 1 importer of the legacy file (`lib/memory.ts`) BEFORE T3.1's redirect; post-redirect grep returned 0 + demo `npm run build` PASS. Legacy `examples/chat-with-memory/lib/openai-adapters.ts` DELETED in T3.2 — single source of truth = SDK per ADR D4. `npm test` baseline preserved: 581 PASS / 2 skipped / 0 type errors (+8 from the 573 baseline = T1.1 smoke 1 + T1.2 smoke 1 + EC-3 guard 2 + exports embedders 2 + exports llms 2). `/deps-audit` PASS_WITH_CAVEATS (zero CVE on openai at any 4.x version; 4 pre-existing MODERATE allowlisted via drizzle-kit esbuild chain unchanged; major bumps v5/v6 deferred to Cycle 2.X per ADR D3). Plan: `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/adapters-openai-cycle-2-plan.md`.

- **Deterministic stub adapters (`@experimental` — debug only)** at `@usetheo/memory/embedders/stub` + `@usetheo/memory/llms/stub` (`adapters-stub-embedder-llm-cycle-1-plan.md` v1.1 SHIPPABLE 96.8 — Roadmap Phase II Cycle 1; Adapters portfolio entry P0). `DeterministicStubEmbedder` implements the `Embedder` DIP via MD5-seeded Lehmer LCG (Park-Miller MINSTD a=48271, m=2^31-1) producing 1536-dim L2-normalized vectors with EC-1 NaN-guard fallback (zero-vector when pre-norm magnitude < 1e-30; defends pgvector(1536) INSERT against degenerate-MD5 NaN crash). `DeterministicStubLLM` implements the `LLM` DIP via MD5-tagged chat (`stub-response:<hex>` per ADR D3) + AdditiveExtractionSchema-conformant `generateResponse({type:'json'})` returning `{ memory: [{ id, text, ... }] }` (locked by SEPA pre-RED brief over the plan's stale `{ facts: [...] }` guess — implementation matches the real schema at `src/core/extraction/prompt.ts:89`). Both adapters are PURE FUNCTIONS — same input → byte-identical output across runs; unlocks F-T4.1-2 race-condition reproducibility debugging WITHOUT external API dep. Ships behind 4 new subpath exports in `package.json#exports` (`./embedders`, `./embedders/stub`, `./llms`, `./llms/stub`) following ADR D4 discipline: deterministic classes are NOT root re-exported (consumers must opt-in via subpath; regression-guarded by `src/__tests__/no-root-reexport.test.ts` EC-6 absorption). Opt-in demo wiring: `THEOMEM_USE_STUB_ADAPTERS=1` env var swaps `OpenAIEmbedder`/`OpenAILLM` for the deterministic stubs in `examples/chat-with-memory/lib/memory.ts` (ADR D5; strict `=== "1"` equality; console log surfaces `adapters=stub` for visibility). Pre-existing zero-vector probe-safe stubs at `src/local/stubs.ts` (v0.2 ADR D8) STAY INTACT — two stub flavors coexist by design (probe-safe vs deterministic-debug). Zero new npm deps (Node built-in `crypto` + textbook LCG; Inquebrável Rule 9 honored). **Fix-up cycle:** `adapters-stub-embedder-llm-cycle-1-fixes-plan.md` v1.1 (SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 84.4) absorbs the /review verdict NEEDS_FIXES (1 CRITICAL F-wire-1 — wiring pillar b via 2 ADR-DEFER-WIRING-B markers landed in the source impl contract + 5 MAJOR + 8 MINOR) + 11 edge cases. Plan: `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/adapters-stub-embedder-llm-cycle-1-plan.md`.

- **v0.6 — `memory.skills(repoId)` code-as-skill procedural memory layer** (Roadmap Item 8; THE GOAL TEST anchor `tests/integration/skills/consolidate-two-skills.test.ts`): new public surface for code-agent skill persistence with `derived_from` provenance + supersession-based versioning. **API:** `memory.skills(repoId)` returns `ScopedSkillMemory` with 4 verbs — `add(skill: SkillDraft)` writes `kind='skill'` rows with code-as-skill payload, `recall(query, options?)` semantic search with `onlySkills` + `includeSuperseded` filters (default `false` — returns tip-of-chain), `consolidate(options?)` runs the 4-phase Orient → Gather → Consolidate → Report orchestrator producing one derived skill per cluster of semantically-equivalent sources, `forget(skillId)` hard-deletes via store. **Architecture:** REUSE of the v0.4a `executeReflection` + `validateProvenance` + `insertDerivedSkill` chain (no sibling chain per plan ADR D1); additive `kind='skill'` discriminator + `superseded_by` self-FK + 4 new memories columns (code/description/code_language) shipped via Drizzle migration `0002_skill_columns.sql` (EC-1 absorption: CHECK constraint name `memories_kind_derived_from_check` cited verbatim in DROP+ADD pair). **Atomicity** (EC-2 absorption): `insertDerivedSkillAtomic(args, sourceIds)` on PgvectorStore wraps INSERT + UPDATE in a single BEGIN/COMMIT — sources are marked `superseded_by` atomically with the derived skill insert. In-memory store runs the pair sequentially as documented best-effort (tests/dev only). **Filter parity** (EC-3 absorption): `Filters` gains `kind` + `excludeSuperseded`; `RecallOptions` + `GetAllOptions` gain `onlySkills` + `includeSuperseded`; Orient phase calls with both options for consistent semantics across recall and getAll. **Scope** (EC-4 absorption): new `RepoScope` ({kind:'repo', repoId}) on the Scope union; 4 switch sites updated (`filtersFromScope`, `assertScopeIsNonEmpty`, `scopeToCounterTuple` in threshold-reflection-policy, scope identifier formatting in core/reflection/prompt). RepoScope.repoId persists in `agent_id` column; `kind='skill'` discriminates from regular agent memories sharing the same id. **DIP discipline:** `insertSkill` / `insertDerivedSkill` / `markSuperseded` / `insertDerivedSkillAtomic` stay duck-typed on local stores (NOT on the VectorStore DIP) — matches the v0.4a `insertDerivedMemory` precedent; external adapters not required to implement skill semantics. **OTel:** consolidate emits parent span `skills.consolidate` + 4 child spans (orient / gather / consolidate / report) with phase-level attributes when a tracer is provided. **Public exports:** `Skill`, `SkillDraft`, `SkillWithScore`, `SkillRecallOptions`, `ConsolidateOptions`, `ConsolidationResult`, `ScopedSkillMemory`, `SkillLanguage`, `SourceCluster`, `RepoScope`, `SkillRepresentation` + `SkillRepresentationStrategy` (DIP slot reserved per blueprint Rec #2 for future natural-language-spec representation), `InvalidSkillError`. All reachable through both `@usetheo/memory` and `@usetheo/memory/contract`. **Plan:** `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/skill-library-plan.md` v1.1 (SHIPPABLE 92.8/100 per `/plan-confidence`). **Blueprint:** `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/skill-library-blueprint.md` v1.0 (SHIPPABLE_WITH_CAVEATS 89.0/100). **No new deps** required.
- **Per-plan paired knowledge skills for auto-generated agents** (cycle-implement v1.4 + cycle-review v1.1, 2026-05-25): every auto-generated agent (SEPA from `/implement`, 5-7 review agents from `/review`) now also generates a **paired knowledge skill** at `.claude/skills/{agent-name}-knowledge/SKILL.md`. The skill is Claude Code Skills-spec-conformant ([docs](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills)) — YAML frontmatter (name, description "pushy", `allowed-tools: Read Glob Grep WebSearch WebFetch`, `model: opus`) + body that hydrates plan-specific context (Goal, ADRs, edge-case findings absorbed, project rules) + instructs invocation modes: cheap plan-context recap, WebSearch community knowledge refresh (allowlisted via `discover-web-allowlist.txt`: martinfowler.com, refactoring.guru, sourcemaking.com, etc.), cross-reference grep. **Why:** without paired skills, agents cite community best practices from training-data recall — stale (TypeScript idioms 2024 ≠ 2026; MCP spec evolved) + un-citable. With paired skills, agents invoke `Skill` tool and get verbatim quotes from canonical sources. Honors Inquebrável Rule 3 (extreme honesty) + cycle-discover.md v1.1 Anti-pattern #10 (no training-data citations when WebSearch is available). User flagged 2026-05-25: "Crie conforme especificou Per-plan". 6 new templates (`sepa-knowledge-skill-template.md` + 5 review skill templates: architecture, tests, wiring, cross-validation, domain) + `spawn_reviewers.py` extended with `write_skill_file` + `--skills-dir` + `--no-skills` flags + 4 new tests (18/18 PASS). Rotation follows the agent lifecycle (move to `_archive/` after 30 days post PR-merge).

### Added

- **`createLocalMemory({ eviction: {...} })` opt-in factory wiring for v0.4b eviction** (v0.4b-fixes T4.1; closes F-dom-2 CRITICAL + F-arch-5 + F-wire-2 + F-xval-3 MAJOR per ADR D6). `LocalMemoryConfig.eviction?: LocalEvictionConfig` discriminated union accepts `{ trigger: 'capacity', maxMemoriesPerScope }` OR `{ trigger: 'time', cronExpression, evictionFraction, scope }`. The factory constructs the `PgEvictor` + `PgHalfLifePersister` + `PgNearestNeighborProvider` chain + wires a private `postRememberHook` (capacity path) or starts the cron (time path). Return type widened from `Memory` to `DisposableMemory = Memory & { dispose: Promise<void> }` so consumers can stop the cron cleanly; non-eviction memories receive a no-op `dispose` for unconditional `await memory.dispose`. New error `EvictionNotSupported` is thrown when `eviction` is set with `InMemoryVectorStore` (advisory locks + pgvector cosine distance are PG-only — fail-fast at the boundary per architecture.md § 8). New public barrel `src/local/eviction/index.ts` re-exports the v0.4b classes + types + `scope-where.ts` helpers; the root `src/index.ts` re-exports the same surface so consumers can construct pipelines manually too. 4 RED-then-GREEN unit tests at `src/local/__tests__/eviction-barrel.test.ts` + 5 RED-then-GREEN integration tests at `tests/integration/forgetting/createLocalMemory-eviction.test.ts` (against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`) including the EC-6 absorption test (concurrent capacity-via-factory + time-via-manual triggers on the same scope share the kind-namespaced advisory lock — total soft-deletes ≤ overflow_count).

### Changed

- **MINOR cluster cleanup (5 cheap fixes)** (v0.4b-fixes T12.1; closes F-arch-6 + F-xval-2 + F-dom-db-5 + F-dom-pg-5 + F-dom-pg-6 MINOR). (1) F-xval-2: `CapacityBasedTrigger` + `TimeBasedTrigger` now invoke the `EvictionPolicy.onEvictionStart` + `onEvictionEnd` lifecycle hooks declared by ADR D12 — previously declared on the DIP but never called. Trigger `policy` field type widened from concrete `TriadicScoringPolicy` to the `EvictionPolicy` DIP so the optional hooks are reachable + v0.5+ alternative policies can plug in. Hooks are best-effort: thrown errors are swallowed (observability must NOT poison eviction). 2 RED-then-GREEN integration tests at `tests/integration/eviction/lifecycle-hooks.test.ts`. (2) F-arch-6: `console.warn` in `TimeBasedTrigger.start`'s tick-failure path now carries an `@todo v0.5` marker pointing at the planned `src/core/dip/logger.ts` (no structured logger interface exists at v0.4b; console.warn stays as the diagnostic channel until then). (3) F-dom-db-5: `0003_eviction_columns.sql` trailing newline already shipped via T0.1 — verified. (4) F-dom-pg-5: `advisoryLockKey` JSDoc extended with a paragraph explaining the 32-bit `hashtext` keyspace (~`2^32` distinct keys; bounded blast radius via per-tx `_xact_` lock; no data-corruption risk). (5) F-dom-pg-6: `lazy-half-life.test.ts:238` already uses `ctid::text` (not `xmin`) — verified; JSDoc explains the precision rationale.
- **Deterministic hashes + Fisher-Yates shuffle in v0.4b test fixtures + eval runner** (v0.4b-fixes T11.2; closes F-tests-1 + F-tests-2 MAJOR + EC-8 absorption). New shared helper at `tests/integration/forgetting/fixtures/deterministic-hash.ts` exports `makeSeededRandom(seed)` (Linear Congruential Generator with Numerical Recipes constants), `shuffleDeterministic(input, seed)` (Fisher-Yates with seeded RNG — byte-identical output for the same seed across runs), and `makeHashCounter(prefix)` (stateful monotonic suffix generator). `tests/integration/forgetting/fixtures/rare-target-seed.ts` drops the `Math.random.toString(36).slice(2)` suffix from cluster + rare-target hashes — the per-test-prefix conventions (`h-t7-1-cluster-${c}-${m}`, `h-t7-1-rare-${r}`) are already unique within a test AND reproducible across runs. `docs/forgetting-evaluation/run.ts` swaps `Array.sort( => Math.random - 0.5)` (not a uniform shuffle per the README disclaimer) for `shuffleDeterministic(allIds, 0x4b_11_2)` so the random baseline produces the same recall@10 byte value across runs. 7 RED-then-GREEN unit tests at `tests/integration/forgetting/fixtures/__tests__/deterministic-hash.test.ts` lock the contract (same-seed identity, mutation-free shuffle, different-seed divergence, monotonic hash counter, counter independence).
- **`npm run db:repair-v0-4b-partial-state` for cross-version journal recovery + `runMigrations` idempotency invariant** (v0.4b-fixes T11.1b; closes F-dom-db-3 MAJOR + EC-4 reversal absorption). New script at `scripts/db-repair-v0-4b-partial-state.mjs` detects 3 DB states (`fresh`, `aligned`, `partial-state`) and either runs no-op OR inserts the missing `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` rows for 0003-0006 when consumers applied 0003 via `psql -f` between v0.4b shipping and v0.4b-fixes shipping. Ambiguous states abort with a pointer at `docs/RUNBOOK.md § 4` for manual recovery. Dry-run by default; pass `-- --apply` to perform the repair. New integration tests at `tests/integration/migrations/idempotency.test.ts` (runMigrations idempotent across 2 calls; column-count + journal-count unchanged) + `repair-partial-state.test.ts` (3 tests: detect partial state + restore journal; fresh-DB diagnosis; aligned-DB diagnosis). `IF NOT EXISTS` guards on `0003_eviction_columns.sql` STAY per the EC-4 reversal absorption — the guards are the defensive line that makes both forward migration AND rollback safe regardless of partial-state.
- **v0.4b rollback runbook published at `docs/RUNBOOK.md`** (v0.4b-fixes T11.1a; closes F-dom-db-4 MAJOR + EC-4 absorption — runbook ships FIRST before T11.1b idempotency test + repair script). On-call operators get the SQL block to drop the v0.4b eviction column/trigger/index/index-recreation artifacts atomically, the journal-entry cleanup procedure for `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations`, verification queries, AND the cross-version partial-state recovery instructions for consumers who applied 0003 manually via psql between v0.4b shipping and v0.4b-fixes shipping.
- **PgEvictor OTel counter (`theo.memory.eviction.soft_deletes_total`) now lazy-resolved on first `evict`** (v0.4b-fixes T10.1; closes F-dom-6 MAJOR). Pre-T10.1 the counter was materialized at module load via `metrics.getMeter('@usetheo/memory')`, capturing the no-op default `MeterProvider` permanently when consumers registered their provider AFTER importing `@usetheo/memory` (the canonical sequence when boot order is `createLocalMemory` then `metrics.setGlobalMeterProvider(...)`). The counter now defers to the first `evict` call so consumer-side telemetry wires up correctly. Cached after the first non-empty evict per OTel guidance. Test-only `__resetCounterCacheForTesting` exposed for the unit test that exercises the lazy path; production paths never call it.
- **Eviction partial indexes drop dead `last_accessed_at` key column** (v0.4b-fixes T9.1; closes F-dom-pg-1 MAJOR; HNSW-friendly NN refactor F-dom-pg-2 DEFERRED to v0.5 per EC-7 — see followup `F-T9.1-1`). Migration `0006_drop_dead_index_columns.sql` drops + recreates the 3 eviction partial indexes (`idx_memories_eviction_score`, `idx_memories_eviction_score_user`, `idx_memories_eviction_score_session`) keyed only on the scope-tier column (was `(scope_id, last_accessed_at)`). `last_accessed_at` was dead index weight — no eviction query uses ORDER BY on it (TriadicScoringPolicy computes the eviction order in-app, not via SQL). Schema migration is index-shape-only; smaller index footprint with no query-plan regression (verified empirically: 56/56 eviction+forgetting integration tests + GOAL TEST recall@10 = 1.000 in 12.47s).
- **BREAKING**: `Memory.forget(id)` now soft-deletes via `t_invalid` (consistent with auto-eviction); raw `SELECT * FROM memories WHERE id = $1` post-forget returns 1 row with `t_invalid IS NOT NULL` (v0.1 returned 0 rows). Consumer assertions of `count == 0 after forget` must migrate to `count_where_t_invalid_null == 0`. Historical rows recoverable via `WHERE t_invalid IS NOT NULL`. Same applies to v0.6 `ScopedSkillMemory.forget` which routes through `Memory.forget` via the `LocalScopedSkillMemory.forgetDelegate`. **GDPR escape hatch preserved**: pass `{ hard: true }` to `forget(id, options)` for physical delete (matches the long-documented `ForgetOptions.hard` contract at `src/core/options.ts`). `InMemoryVectorStore` consumers (dev/test) keep the v0.1 hard-delete behavior — `t_invalid` is a PostgreSQL-only concept. Refactor closes F-dom-3 + F-wire-1 MAJOR from `/review v0-4b-forgetting-pipeline-2026-05-30` per ADR D7 declaration. v0.4b-fixes T7.1.

### Fixed

- **Shared `scope-candidate.ts` helper extracted from capacity + time triggers** (v0.4b-fixes T6.1; closes F-arch-3 MAJOR + resolves v0.4b F4 followup). The `ScopeCandidateRow` interface + `rowToMemoryItem` mapper + new `loadCandidates(client, scope)` SQL helper now live in `src/local/eviction/scope-candidate.ts`. `capacity-based-trigger.ts` shrinks from 179 → 129 LoC (-50); `time-based-trigger.ts` from 235 → 184 LoC (-51); both delegate candidate-fetch through the shared helper. T7.1's forget soft-delete path becomes the third consumer when it lands.
- **`HalfLifePersister` + `NearestNeighborProvider` DIP interfaces relocated to `src/core/dip/eviction-policy.ts`** (v0.4b-fixes T5.1; closes F-arch-2 MAJOR). Restores DIP direction per `architecture.md § 3` — the concrete `TriadicScoringPolicy` depends on the abstractions; the abstractions must NOT live alongside the concrete. Both interfaces are now re-exported from `src/core/index.ts` (alongside `EvictionPolicy` + `EvictionResult` + `ScoreContext`), from `src/local/eviction/index.ts` (T4.1's barrel — preserves backward compat for in-tree consumers), and via `src/local/eviction/triadic-scoring-policy.ts` (legacy re-export for any external consumers still importing from that path). `PgHalfLifePersister` + `PgNearestNeighborProvider` + the `SpyNeighborProvider` in `tests/integration/eviction/multiplier-integration.test.ts` updated to import from the canonical `../../core/dip/eviction-policy.js` path; acceptance grep at plan T5.1 line 537 (`grep "import.*HalfLifePersister" src/`) now shows zero matches outside the canonical location. 2 new RED-then-GREEN unit tests at `src/core/__tests__/dip-eviction-policy.test.ts` lock the contract.
- **v0.4b eviction triggers + long-tail multiplier + NN provider now honor all 3 scope tiers (User/Session/Agent)** (v0.4b-fixes T3.1; closes F-dom-1 CRITICAL + F-dom-pg-3 CRITICAL + F-arch-4 + F-dom-4 MAJOR per ADR D3). `CapacityBasedTrigger.fire(scope: Scope, now)` + `TimeBasedTriggerConfig.scope: Scope` + `PgNearestNeighborProvider.distance(memoryId, scope: Scope)` + `TriadicScoringPolicy.nearestNeighborDistance(memoryId, scope: Scope)` widened from `string` (agent-id-only) to the full `Scope` discriminated union; SQL `WHERE` clauses route per `scope.kind` via the new `src/local/eviction/scope-where.ts` helper (`scopeColumn` / `scopeValue` / `advisoryLockKey` / `scopeWhereClause`). Advisory-lock keys are kind-namespaced (`eviction_${kind}_${value}`) — prevents cross-tier false sharing when `userId === agentId`. New migration `0005_eviction_partial_indexes.sql` adds `idx_memories_eviction_score_user (user_id, last_accessed_at) WHERE t_invalid IS NULL` and `idx_memories_eviction_score_session (session_id, last_accessed_at) WHERE t_invalid IS NULL` with `IF NOT EXISTS` guards (EC-4 REVERSAL parity); the existing `idx_memories_eviction_score (agent_id, ...)` is unchanged pending T9.1. **EC-5 absorption:** `getNeighborScopeId` is exported and dispatches on `scope.kind` exhaustively (user/session/agent/repo) — the kind is authoritative, columns are derived. 5 RED-then-GREEN unit tests at `src/local/eviction/__tests__/get-neighbor-scope-id.test.ts` + 5 RED-then-GREEN integration tests at `tests/integration/eviction/multi-tier-eviction.test.ts` (against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`) lock the contract; 7 existing integration test files migrated to the new signatures atomically per ADR D1. `npm test` 531/533 + `npm run test:integration -- tests/integration/eviction tests/integration/forgetting` 44/44 PASS + typecheck + lint clean.

### Changed

Core `ConsolidateOptions.clusterThreshold` default of `0.85` is UNTOUCHED — only the demo's request body changes. **(T2.1, commit `8d5accb`)** README "Notes on the integration" gains 2 bullets explaining both choices + the production-guidance alternative (`ThresholdReflectionPolicy` for persistent counters). **(T3.1)** `/test-chat-with-memory` re-run verdict on 2026-05-30: **`READY` (30/30 PASS)** — report at `.claude/knowledge-base/reviews/chat-with-memory-e2e-2026-05-30-rerun.md`; **D4** transitions FAIL→PASS (`sourceClustersCount: 1, derivedSkillsCount: 1, supersededIds: [1c8fae38, b1c2ede4]` — clusterThreshold 0.6 cleared the OpenAI text-embedding-3-small cosine); **F3** transitions FAIL→PASS (Reflection tile shows `fired` + blue banner visible after 2 messages — HMR-safe globalThis pattern survives Next.js dev-mode module reloads); zero uncaught console exceptions; zero new FAILs in groups A/B/C/D/E/F/G. **Zero changes under `src/`** — the `@usetheo/memory` contract is untouched; the v0.6.1 `/review` `READY_TO_MERGE` verdict + 490 contract tests stay valid. File renamed `sepa-staff-engineer.md` → `sepa.md`. ### Deprecated

- **`TriadicScoringPolicy` 5-positional constructor — use the options-object form instead** (v0.4b-fixes T1.1; closes F-arch-1 CRITICAL per ADR D5). The recommended shape is `new TriadicScoringPolicy({ weights, constants, halfLifePersister, neighborProvider, evictor })` — all fields optional with v0.4b defaults. The legacy `new TriadicScoringPolicy(weights, constants, halfLifePersister, neighborProvider, evictor)` signature is preserved as `@deprecated` for one release; EC-2 absorption ensures `undefined` AND `null` first-arg call sites do NOT throw TypeError (the naive `'weights' in arg` discriminator would). 12 in-tree call sites migrated to the options-object shape; 6 RED tests in `src/local/eviction/__tests__/triadic-scoring-options.test.ts` lock both signatures.

- **v0.3 T5.1 — 4 dispatch handlers + central dispatch wired to `tools/call`** (Phase 5 integration; EC-6 empty-recall + EC-14 idempotent forget + 2 SEPA-flagged contract gotchas absorbed): 4 handler files (`{remember,recall,forget,reflect}-handler.ts`) + `dispatch.ts` central Map<TOOL_NAME, handler> lookup. Each handler: zod validate args (T2.1) → resolve scoped memory via `memoryFromCtx(getScope, deps.memoryFactory)` (T3.1+v0.2 PR-A) → call ScopedMemory method (v0.1) → return CallToolResult. Dispatch wraps each call in try/catch routing through T4.1 `classifyError` → Protocol Errors THROW `McpError` (SDK serializes to JSON-RPC `error{}`); Tool Execution Errors RETURN CallToolResult with `isError:true` (SDK serializes to JSON-RPC `result{}`) per ADR D6. **2 SEPA pre-RED CRITICAL contract gotchas absorbed**: (1) `forget` is on TOP-LEVEL `Memory`, NOT `ScopedMemory` (per `src/core/memory-store.ts:60`) — handler calls `deps.memoryFactory.forget(id)` directly while still gating on `getScope` for invariant; (2) `Memory.forgetAll` does NOT exist — `all: true` short-circuits to Tool Execution Error "bulk delete deferred to v0.4" BEFORE any Memory call. EC-6 absorbed: empty-recall returns `{content: [{text: 'No memories matched the query.'}], structuredContent: {results: []}, isError: false}` (NEVER empty `content: []`). EC-14 absorbed: `Memory.forget(unknownId)` throws `MemoryNotFound` (v0.1 EC-12 divergence from ); handler catches → idempotent success per REST DELETE semantics. Recall createdAt:Date → ISO string mapper at wire boundary. McpServerOptions extended with optional `memoryFactory:  => Memory`. server.ts wires `setRequestHandler(CallToolRequestSchema, ...)` with absent-factory fallback returning Tool Execution Error. 12 unit tests at `src/agent-tools/tools/__tests__/handlers.test.ts` (BDD `it` per testing.md): 4 happy paths + EC-6 + createdAt mapping + EC-14 + scope rejection + invalid args + unknown tool + MemoryStoreError propagation. eslint.config.ts + tsconfig.contract.json `include` extended to cover `src/agent-tools/**` (was missing — lint silently skipped these files since T0.1). Full contract suite 305/305 (+12 from T5.1). 0 lint errors. F-T4.1-3 EXPECTED_DOMAIN_ERRORS deferred to future observability (no T5.1 consumer; classifier-only YAGNI). Wiring (b) ADR-DEFER to T7.1 E2E.
- **v0.3 T4.1 — error classifier + 3 new domain error classes** (Phase 4; EC-5 PG_TRANSIENT_CODES + EC-13 stack-leak guard absorbed): `src/agent-tools/error-classifier.ts` ships `classifyError(err): {kind: 'protocol' | 'execution', payload}` per ADR D6 (TWO MCP wire-shapes). Tagged union maps domain errors to Tool Execution Errors (`isError: true` + content text — LLM-self-correctable); maps `UnknownToolError` to JSON-RPC `-32601 Method not found` (standard message; details in `data.details`); maps `ZodError` (boundary validation fail) to `-32602 Invalid params` with `data.issues`; fallback `-32603 Internal error` sanitizes — Error.cause + Error.stack NEVER leaked into wire (EC-13 verified via JSON.stringify substring check). EC-5 absorbed: `PG_TRANSIENT_CODES = Set(['ECONNREFUSED', 'ETIMEDOUT', 'ENOTFOUND', 'EPIPE', 'ECONNRESET'])` — pg driver transient errors map to Tool Execution Error with "Retry in a moment" actionable text (-style graceful-degradation pattern per blueprint Q5 lines 46-77). `UnknownToolError` sentinel class introduced (single-file scope; T5.1 dispatch throws when `tools/call` names a tool outside the locked verb set). 3 new domain error classes added to `src/core/errors.ts` (`MemoryStoreError`, `EmbedderError`, `ContentTooLargeError`) following the v0.2 PR-A `setPrototypeOf` + `override readonly name as const` pattern for cross-module instanceof survival. `EXPECTED_DOMAIN_ERRORS` catalog exported per blueprint Q3 pattern (4-entry tuple for future observability log-level classification). 8 unit tests at `src/agent-tools/__tests__/error-classifier.test.ts` (BDD `it` per testing.md). Full contract suite 293/293 (+8 from T4.1). DIP boundary clean. Wiring (b) ADR-DEFER to T5.1 dispatch + T7.1 E2E JSON-RPC roundtrip.
- **v0.3 T3.1 — AsyncLocalStorage scope binding + `ScopedContext` core move** (Phase 3; EC-4 HARD GATE + EC-12 STRICT absorbed): `src/agent-tools/scope.ts` ships `withScope(ctx, fn)` runner + `getScope` retriever via Node native `AsyncLocalStorage` from `node:async_hooks` (auto-resets via lexical scope, no manual try/finally per ADR D3). `ScopedContext` interface moved from `src/theokit/types.ts` to `src/core/scope.ts` (single source of truth consumed by both `memoryFromCtx` v0.2 PR-A + agent-tools scope binding v0.3); theokit re-exports via `@see import` form preserving v0.2 ADR D1/D4/D6 JSDoc citations. **EC-4 HARD GATE verified**: 50/50 integration suite GREEN against real pgvector (incl. 5/5 `tests/integration/theokit-binding.test.ts` from v0.2 PR-A) AFTER the ScopedContext relocation — backward compat preserved. EC-12 + SEPA-added STRICT invariant: `withScope` rejects null/undefined ctx at entry (throws `MissingScopeError`), nested `withScope(outer,  => withScope(undefined, ...))` throws immediately. 7 unit tests at `src/agent-tools/__tests__/scope.test.ts` cover: throws-outside / returns-inside / nested-inner-wins / **concurrent zero-overlap via Promise.all with await** (forces V8 microtask interleaving — guards against false positives) / type-import-from-core / EC-12 nested-undefined-STRICT / SEPA-added null+undefined-entry-STRICT. Full contract suite 285/285 (+7 from T3.1). Wiring (b) ADR-DEFER to T7.1 E2E JSON-RPC roundtrip.
- **v0.3 T2.2 — `tools/list` response emitter + ADR D9 schema-emit helper** (Phase 2 wire-up; EC-3 + F-T2.1-3 absorbed): `src/agent-tools/tools/schema-emit.ts` ships `ZOD_TO_JSON_SCHEMA_TARGET = 'jsonSchema7' as const` (single source of truth per ADR D9) + `emitToolSchema(zodSchema)` helper centralizing dialect choice across 4 tool inputSchemas + recall outputSchema. `src/agent-tools/tools/list-handler.ts` ships `buildToolsListResponse: ListToolsResult` returning 4 tools in CLAUDE.md § Locked names declaration order (`remember/recall/forget/reflect`), no `nextCursor` (4 static tools per ADR D2 `listChanged:false`). Wired into `McpServer` constructor via `sdkServer.setRequestHandler(ListToolsRequestSchema,  => Promise.resolve(buildToolsListResponse))`. 5 unit tests at `src/agent-tools/tools/__tests__/list-handler.test.ts` cover: exact tool count (4 + no nextCursor), locked-verb declaration order, F-zod-schema-fixture-test (remember.inputSchema.required includes 'content' — proves `_ToolWithRequired` workaround unneeded per blueprint Q6), recall has non-empty outputSchema with type:'object', **F-T2.1-3 absorption: reflect.inputSchema.additionalProperties === false** (proves `target='jsonSchema7'` translates `.strict` correctly). Full contract suite 278/278 (+5 from T2.2). Wiring (b) ADR-DEFER to T7.1 E2E JSON-RPC roundtrip.
- **v0.3 T2.1 — 4 zod schemas + tool metadata** (Phase 2; EC-2 + EC-10 + EC-11 absorbed inline): `src/agent-tools/tools/{remember,recall,forget,reflect}-schema.ts` author one zod `INPUT_SCHEMA` per verb + `TOOL_NAME` constant + human-legible `DESCRIPTION`. `recall` additionally ships `OUTPUT_SCHEMA` (typed `structuredContent.results[]` shape — minimal honest subset of `MemoryItemWithScore` from `src/core/memory-item.ts:48`: `{id, text, score, scope, createdAt, metadata}` only; dropped `attributedTo` (v1.1 fabrication), `hash` (internal dedup), `updatedAt` (==createdAt under ADD-only ADR-0003), `tValid`/`tInvalid` (bi-temporal asOf v0.5+ surface)). `recall.topK` defaults to **20** matching v0.0 contract per `src/core/retrieval/pipeline.ts:73` (honors `architecture.md § 5` two surfaces, one contract — plan v1.1 test name "default_topK_5" was transcription error; F-T2.1-1 logged). EC-2 absorption via `tools/constants.ts` `MAX_CONTENT_BYTES = 50_000` + `MAX_QUERY_BYTES = 2_000` applied as `z.string.max` on `remember.content` + `recall.query`. EC-10 absorption: `recall.threshold = z.number.min(0).max(1).default(0)`. EC-11 absorption: `reflect = z.object({}).strict` (extras rejected — prevents silent drift when v0.4a adds `since: timestamp`). `forget` mutual exclusion via `.refine(d => Boolean(d.id) !== Boolean(d.all))` per ADR D6 (refine fail → Protocol Error -32602). 9/9 unit tests at `src/agent-tools/tools/__tests__/schemas.test.ts`. Wiring (a)+(b) ADR-DEFER to T2.2 (list-handler) + T5.1 (dispatch) + T7.1 (E2E roundtrip).
- **v0.3 T1.1 — McpServer wrapper class + capability declaration** (Phase 1 bootstrap): `src/agent-tools/server.ts` ships `McpServer` thin wrapper around `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js#Server`. Constructor declares `capabilities.tools.listChanged: false` (frozen) per ADR D2 — v0.3 ships 4 static tools, no dynamic registration ( skill library lands v0.6 per CLAUDE.md Roadmap Item 8). Defaults: `name='themory'`, `version='0.3.0'` (overridable via `McpServerOptions`). Async lifecycle: `start(transport)` → `sdkServer.connect(transport)`; `stop` → `sdkServer.close`. `createMcpServer(options?)` factory replaces T0.2 throw-stub. 3 unit tests at `src/agent-tools/__tests__/server.test.ts` cover capability declaration + name default + version override (testing.md § "What to test — public API" via readonly getters, no SDK private bracket-access). Wiring pillar (b) ADR-deferred to T6.1+ (transport binding makes integration meaningful) — marker logged in implementation contract.
- **v0.3 T0.2 — `@usetheo/memory/agent-tools` subpath scaffold + bin entry + build pipeline** (EC-1 absorbed): added `./agent-tools` subpath export pointing to `./src/agent-tools/index.ts` + `bin: { "themory-mcp": "./dist/agent-tools/cli.js" }` for `npx @usetheo/memory/agent-tools` invocation per ADR D8 (package surface). New `tsconfig.build.json` extends `tsconfig.contract.json` with `outDir: "./dist"` + `noEmit: false` + `declaration: true` — pure tsc build pipeline (no tsup/esbuild deps per ADR D7 + SEPA initial-brief locked decision). `npm run build` script emits `dist/agent-tools/cli.js` (EC-1 absorption gate verified by 4th integration test). Stub `createMcpServer` throws "deferred to T1.1". Main barrel `src/index.ts` does NOT re-export agent-tools (bundle isolation per ADR D8 tripwire — regex-tightened to allow JSDoc comments). 4/4 new public-path tests + 6/6 publish-readiness regression-clean.
- **v0.2 PR-A — TheoKit binding core (Roadmap Item 3, partial)**: `@usetheo/memory/theokit` subpath exports `memoryFromCtx(ctx, memory)` — auto-scopes memory from any TheoKit-shaped request context via structural typing (zero peer-dep on the `theokit` package per ADR D1). 3-state resolver per ADR D4 with explicit `MissingScopeError` for (b) `ctx.session === null` and (c) `session/userId` absent/empty/whitespace/non-string (EC-3 + EC-6 runtime type-guard; EC-7 no-mutation invariant; EC-8 sessionId/agentId reserved for v0.3+). 10 unit tests + 5 integration tests against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1` proving THE GOAL: two distinct userIds produce DISJOINT recall sets (`expect(intersect).toEqual([])`). T4.1-T4.4 + T5.1 (scaffolded create-theo templates + E2E) land separately in sibling `theokit` repo PR per ADR D7. (plan `v0-2-theokit-binding-plan.md` v1.1)
- `MissingScopeError` typed domain error in `@usetheo/memory/contract` with stable `as const` name + `Object.setPrototypeOf` for EC-2 cross-module instanceof survival; re-exported from `@usetheo/memory/theokit` for ergonomic instanceof checks in error boundaries (T0.1, ADR D4)
- `ScopedContext` structural interface in `@usetheo/memory/theokit` subsuming all 5 TheoKit fixture variants from blueprint Q5 (sessions-auth, template-saas, auth-providers-with-authjs, auth-providers-diy-github, template-postgres) (T0.2, ADR D1)
- `StubEmbedder` + `StubLLM` in `@usetheo/memory` for scaffold-demo defaults (ADR D8 + EC-2 absorption): probe-safe zero-vector embedder + empty-content LLM with single warn-once diagnostic per instance. Scaffolded `npm install && npm run dev` boots without `EmbeddingMismatch` crash; consumer sees demo route work BEFORE wiring real providers. `@experimental` JSDoc on both — replace before production. (T4.5)
- `PROBE_TEXT` constant exported from `@usetheo/memory` (was a private const) — DRY between `createLocalMemory`'s dimension probe and `StubEmbedder`'s probe-aware path (T4.5)
- `publish:check` npm script as the publish-readiness gate runnable locally AND from any future CI workflow; the `--tag next` is mandatory because npm rejects dry-run of a pre-release version (0.0.0-rc.X) without explicit tag (T2.3, ADR D8)
- Integration tests at `tests/integration/publish-readiness.test.ts` (6 tests): package.json shape + tarball excludes test artifacts + POSITIVE inclusion of every declared `package.json#exports` backing source (F1 absorption — prevents silent "published-but-missing" failure mode) (T2.3 + T4.5)
- Integration tests at `tests/integration/public-paths.test.ts` (7 tests): subpath resolution + bundle isolation (ADR D6 double-assertion: runtime keys + static-source tripwire on `src/index.ts`) + EC-9 absorption (subpath compiles under TypeScript `moduleResolution: "node16" | "bundler" | "nodenext"` via spawned tsc against ESM tmpdir fixtures) (T2.1)
- Integration tests at `tests/integration/theokit-binding.test.ts` (5 tests): THE GOAL isolation assertion + state (a)/(b)/(c) resolver branches + F5 empty-scope usability (recall returns `[]` cleanly when scope has no memories) (T3.1)
- **v0.1 — Core + local runtime (Roadmap Item 2)**: 8-phase write pipeline + 9-step search pipeline + MD5 hash dedup + Porter/simpleStem lemmatization + additive scoring + per-row I/O fallback at every boundary + OpenTelemetry spans per phase + bi-temporal Drizzle schema (memories + entities + history) + HNSW pgvector index (1536-dim cap per ADR D1.7) + two `VectorStore` adapters (in-memory + pgvector raw SQL via `pg`) + `DrizzleHistoryManager` for audit trail + `createMemory` factory wiring all 5 DIPs + public barrel at `src/index.ts` + canonical end-to-end integration test (`memory.user('alice').remember('Paulo likes coffee') + recall('beverages')` returns the memory in top-3 against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`). Closes Roadmap Item 2. (#PR-8, plan `v0-1-local-runtime-plan.md` v1.3)
- Implementation plan `memory-contract-v0-0-plan.md` v1.3 for Roadmap Item 1 (v0.0 Contract design). Five phases, 14 tasks, 9 ADRs (D1-D9 ratifying CLAUDE.md locked decisions + resolving Open questions Q2-Q6). Coverage Matrix 31/31. Verdict via `/plan-confidence`: SHIPPABLE 100/100.

- README Quickstart section covering local-runtime setup + minimum TypeScript usage + v0.1 constraints (1536-dim cap, `MemoryNotFound` on `forget(unknown)`, `InvalidScope` on `reset({})`, `BiTemporalNotSupportedLocally` on `recall({asOf})`, `ReflectionNotConfigured` on `reflect`) (#PR-8 T8.2)
- ADR-0001 § Implementation note (v0.1 runtime) acknowledging the 8-phase code reality (folds to 6-phase narrative for marketing brevity); `architecture.md § 4` Adopt bullet updated in the same PR (#PR-8 T7.3)

### Changed

- `package.json#exports` map gains the `"./theokit"` subpath pointing at `./src/theokit/index.ts` per ADR D6 — separate from the main `.` barrel so consumers NOT using TheoKit pay zero binding bundle cost. The default `@usetheo/memory` barrel does NOT re-export `memoryFromCtx` (verified via runtime keys assertion + static-source tripwire on `src/index.ts`). (T2.1)
- `package.json` `"private": true` removed (T2.3, ADR D8): enables `npm publish --dry-run` validation locally and from CI. **NOT a production-ready claim** — the v1.0 production claim remains gated by `/dogfood` per CLAUDE.md § Honesty discipline. Pre-release publish requires `--tag next`.
- `README.md` TheoKit-native binding section replaced (T2.2): the previous example used pre-ADR-D4 `memory.recall(body, {user, session, agent, scopes})` shape that never shipped. Replaced with the actual shipped T1.1 API (`memoryFromCtx(ctx, memory) + requireAuth(ctx.session)`) plus explicit documentation of the 3-state resolver + EC-11 future-tier deferral (v0.2 resolves `userId` only) + honest framing of default-on scaffolded templates as "planned" (PR-B follow-up).
- `tsconfig.contract.json` + `vitest.contract.config.ts` + `eslint.config.ts` + `package.json` lint script scope all extended to span `src/{core,local,theokit}/**` — necessary infrastructure prerequisite for type-checking, testing, and linting the new binding module (T0.2)
- `eslint.config.ts` test-files block: added `'@typescript-eslint/unbound-method': 'off'` because `vitest-mock-extended`'s `mock<T>` method-spy pattern surfaces unbound-method false-positives. Removed two now-redundant per-line `eslint-disable-next-line` comments in `src/core/__tests__/extraction-pipeline.test.ts` (T1.1)
- `package.json` version bumped to `0.0.0-rc.2`; `_v0_0_exports_preview_only` flag removed; `exports` map gains the `"."` entry (default subpath now resolves to `src/index.ts`) while preserving the `"./contract"` subpath for backward compatibility with v0.0 consumers (#PR-8 T0.1 + T7.2)
- Runtime dependencies added: `pg ^8.13.0`, `drizzle-orm ^0.45.0` (per ADR D1.10 forced bump for HIGH CVE — supersedes the earlier ADR D1.9 pin at ^0.36), `zod ^3.24.0` (pinned per ADR D1.8 — v4 migration deferred to v0.2 ecosystem readiness) (#PR-8 T0.1)
- Dev dependencies added: `drizzle-kit ^0.31.0` (per ADR D1.10 — peer of drizzle-orm 0.45 + brings patched esbuild ^0.25.4), `@types/pg ^8.11.0`, `vitest-mock-extended ^4.0.0`, `@opentelemetry/api ^1.9.0`, `@opentelemetry/sdk-trace-base ^2.0.0` (#PR-8 T0.1)
- Optional peer dependency added: `natural ^8.1.0` (Porter stemmer for BM25 lemmatization; in-house `simpleStem` fallback when not installed) (#PR-8 T0.1)
- ESLint config widened from `src/core/**/*.ts` to `src/{core,local}/**/*.ts`; `parserOptions.project` bumped to dual `[tsconfig.contract.json, tsconfig.local.json]` to type-check the new local-runtime files (#PR-8 T2.1)
- Plan v1.2 → v1.3: corrected file-location rule violation. Documents (ADRs) relocated from `docs/adrs/` to `.claude/knowledge-base/adrs/` (canonical knowledge-base root per project convention); TypeScript types relocated from `docs/contract/` to `src/core/` (aligns with `architecture.md § 3 Dependency direction` — `src/core/` ← contracts, types, pure logic).

### Security

- `drizzle-orm` bumped from the v1.2-plan-pinned `^0.36.0` to `^0.45.0` (SemVer-major) to clear a HIGH CVE on SQL injection via improperly escaped SQL identifiers (affects all `drizzle-orm < 0.45.2`); discovered during the first real-tree `npm audit` run at /implement T0.1 iteration 1, fixed via plan v1.2 → v1.3 + ADR D1.10. The forced bump retires the earlier ADR D1.9 pinning rationale (API stability cannot override security imperative). (#PR-8 plan v1.3)
- Four MODERATE CVEs on the dev-only `esbuild` chain (chained via drizzle-kit's deprecated `@esbuild-kit/esm-loader`) allowlisted with 90-day sunset (2026-08-23) in `.claude/rules/deps-audit-allowlist.txt`; drizzle-kit is a devDep + dev-server is never spun up in our usage, so the dev-server-leak CVE has no production-runtime impact. Re-audit at sunset to verify either upstream drizzle-kit migration to `tsx` or escalation. (#PR-8 plan v1.3 + audit v3)

### Status

Pre-release. v0.0 contract (Roadmap Item 1) shipped via PR #4. v0.1 core + local runtime (Roadmap Item 2) shipped via PR #8 — 23/23 tasks committed; canonical write→search end-to-end loop validates Roadmap Item 2 acceptance.

**v0.2 PR-A (Roadmap Item 3, partial) in flight on `feat/discover-v0-2-theokit-binding`**: 9/9 PR-A tasks committed (T0.1, T0.2, T1.1, T2.1, T2.2, T2.3, T4.5, T3.1, T6.1); 258 contract tests + 46 integration tests pass against real `ankane/pgvector:v0.5.1`; THE GOAL metric verified via `tests/integration/theokit-binding.test.ts` isolation test (`expect(intersect).toEqual([])`). T4.1-T4.4 + T5.1 (scaffolded `create-theo` templates + E2E) deferred to a sibling `theokit` repo PR per ADR D7 — PR-A must merge + package publish (with `--tag next`) BEFORE PR-B's templates can resolve `@usetheo/memory@^0.1.0-next.0`.

### Known issues

- Drizzle-kit's `history_timestamp_idx` re-applies on every `db:push` due to SQL-expression-based introspection roundtripping limitation. Cosmetic only; production uses `db:generate` + Drizzle migrator at app boot.
- `drizzle-kit push` requires `--force` flag in non-TTY contexts (CI). The `npm run db:push` script does NOT include `--force` by default; documented in README Quickstart.
- v0.1 readiness gate: verify `npm view @usetheo/memory` returns 404 or is owned by the `@usetheo` npm org, and that the publish auth token is provisioned. Not a v0.1 task; flagged for v0.1 publication kickoff.
- v0.2 PR-A scaffolded-templates path requires sibling `theokit` repo PR-B (T4.1-T4.4 + T5.1 per ADR D7). PR-A must merge + publish first so PR-B's `package.json.tmpl` can resolve `@usetheo/memory@^0.1.0-next.0`. Without PR-B, consumers using `npx create-theokit` will NOT get default-on memory yet — they add the dep + wire the singleton manually per README guidance.
- v0.2 F2 followup logged at `.claude/knowledge-base/implementations/v0-2-theokit-binding-followups.md`: real-embedder probe parity test deferred to PR-B (the scaffolded TheoKit handler is the real-embedder integration surface; standalone real-embedder unit test in PR-A would be redundant).
